## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

This is a keynote built for a live speaker, and the visual identity is consistent: black ground, gold and green accents, large product renders and photography, and one short title per slide. The route runs from hardware (6 to 15) through European infrastructure (16 to 27), open models (30 to 36), agents and developer platforms (37 to 48), industrial digital twins (49 to 60) and vehicles and robots (61 to 68), and the closing recap on slide 69 ties it together. Several pages cannot be read by themselves: the busy slide 10 chart with tiny multicolour labels, the small-type partner architecture diagrams on slides 38 and 42 to 48, and the dense logo walls on slides 2, 4 and 61. Slide 10 has no takeaway title, and many slides with a title and a picture rely on the speaker to carry the meaning, so the deck is competent and usable but not self-explanatory.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the consistent dark identity, the large hero renders on slides 13, 14 and 17, the Europe map series on slides 18 to 23 and the clean recap on slide 69.

**Most useful repair:** Enlarge or simplify the small-type architecture diagrams on slides 38 and 42 to 48, and reduce the logo walls on slides 2, 4 and 61 and the labels on slide 10 so they can be read at a glance.

**What would change the score:** I would give slide 10 a stated conclusion and a clearer Hopper versus Blackwell comparison, and repeat the three UK, Germany, France and Italy stack diagrams with a larger shared template.

_Confidence medium. 70 of 70 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck is a keynote given at GTC Paris 2025 and opens with a quantum-GPU computing slide before moving to Blackwell hardware. It then covers reasoning inference performance, the production ramp of Grace Blackwell systems, and a single architecture spanning cloud, enterprise, personal and edge AI. Mid-deck sections address European AI infrastructure, with telcos, cloud providers, supercomputing centres and a list of EU AI factories, followed by open models such as Nemotron, a developer platform called DGX Cloud Lepton, and industrial AI and digital twins for European automotive makers. Autonomous vehicles and robotics follow, with photo-led slides that show partners and products. The deck closes with a summary slide listing its main claims: a thinking machine, AI factories as new infrastructure, tenfold European infrastructure growth over two years, and agents and robots. Slides worth studying include the bar-chart slide comparing tokens per second per megawatt, the regional country-by-country stack diagrams, and the closing summary, which compresses the talk into a few short phrases.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the NVIDIA GTC Paris 2025 keynote deck have?**

The NVIDIA deck has 70 pages. Its structure runs from the opening quantum-GPU slide (slide 4) through Blackwell hardware, European AI infrastructure, open models, autonomous vehicles and robotics, and a closing summary slide.

**What does the NVIDIA deck say about European AI factories?**

Slide 21, titled European Union Building 20 AI Factories, lists 13 named sites including Barcelona Supercomputing Center and CINECA. It also shows 2 AI Factories (Location TBD) and 5 Gigafactories (Location TBD).

**What speedup figures does the NVIDIA deck show for quantum computing with CUDA-Q?**

Slide 5, under the heading NVIDIA CUDA-Q Quantum-GPU Classical Computing, shows a 4,000X Speedup vs. CPU. The same slide also shows 800X, 1,200X, 1,300X and 500X figures.

**What growth projection does the NVIDIA deck give for European AI infrastructure?**

Slide 22 is titled 10X Growth Projection Pan-European AI Infrastructure. It is a column chart with bars for 2024, 2025 and 2026 and a 3X+ label, with GPU Die AI FLOPS as the measure.

**Layout**

A single horizontal row of fifteen-plus rounded-rectangle tiles receding in size from left to right across the middle band of the slide, each tile's label centred beneath it; the row spans roughly 4% to 97% of the width with the tallest tile at left. **Visuals**

Row of software library tiles, each a rounded rectangle with a thin pale gold-beige edge. Left to right: cuLitho tile shows a dark glowing blue-white rack of lithography or computing hardware with cyan light bars and a bright white horizontal strip, dark background, cropped tall. cuDSS AmgX tile shows a glossy dark grey car body with reflections and a scattering of yellow-and-teal particles at bottom. cuOpt tile shows a green glowing node at centre of a blue network of connected white lines and points. Warp tile shows swirling fluid streaks in green, magenta, cyan and blue on black. cuDF cuML tile shows a teal-and-white particle cloud forming a head-and-shoulders silhouette. Dynamo cuDNN tile shows a white flowchart-style block diagram of stacked rectangles and connectors on a deep blue background. cuEquivariance cuTensor tile shows a colourful nebula of pink, orange and blue space imagery. Aerial Sionna tile shows an aerial city view overlaid with bright green radio ray lines and a yellow object. Earth-2 tile shows the Earth from space with clouds and blue oceans. MONAI tile shows a multicoloured segmented medical volume in green, red, pink and yellow. Parabricks tile shows a blue-and-orange protein or DNA molecular structure. cuQuantum CUDA-Q tile shows a gold-toned cryogenic quantum processor cylinder with glowing amber tones. cuPyNumeric tile shows a colourful ball-and-stick molecule with red, blue, teal and white atoms on black. Beneath the row sits a gold-metallic chip package shown as two square dies on a black reflective surface, its reflection fading downward. Tiles are photographs or renders, full-bleed inside their frames, with slight perspective and overlap.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a faint dark reflective floor and blurred coloured light at the bottom edge. Pure black background with a faint blurred row of coloured lights along the lower edge. Labels are set in a small sans-serif regular weight in pale gold-beige #D9C9A3, centred under each tile at about 1.6% of slide height. Tile edges are thin pale gold #CDB98A with rounded corners of about 2% of width. No visible title text, so no title colour. Accent colour is the gold #CDB98A used on edges, labels and the chip.

**Visuals**

Photograph 1 (QPU): a tall rounded-rectangle photo with a thin gold-bronze border, at x 1.9 y 18.8 w 26.1 h 72.9. It shows a gold-lit quantum processor, a dilution-refrigerator stack of gold and amber tiered plates with dangling cabling, under a bright silver-white ring at the top, against a near-black background. It is framed straight-on, boxed rather than full-bleed, with warm amber key light and cool white highlights at the top. Photograph 2 (GB200 NVL72): a tall dark server rack, at x 19.9 y 6.2 w 13.8 h 86.0, seen front-on, cropped by the rectangle edges; its dark grey-black frame holds rows of gold-lit compute trays with cable loops at the top and bottom. It sits behind the QPU photo, which overlaps its left side. Logos: 34 partner logos listed in the elements, all in white or grey on black, no coloured logos. No charts, tables or diagrams.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a full-bleed image of the same black beneath everything (white fill underneath it). Pure black full-bleed background with the slide photo beneath. Title and captions are white NVIDIA Sans style sans-serif; the title is medium weight, the captions regular. Logos are white or mid grey (about #8C8C8C to #D0D0D0) and vary in weight. Title text is about 3.7 percent of slide height, captions about 1.7 percent. No shapes, lines, shadows or gradients except the thin gold border on the QPU photo.

**Layout**

Two zones on a black field. The left zone (about x 2 to 47) holds a rounded QPU photograph overlapping the front-left of a tall GB200 NVL72 server rack, with the captions QPU and GB200 NVL72 beneath them. The right zone (about x 42 to 94) holds a centred two-line title at the top and a horizontal gold timeline across the lower third, with five circular icons, rising value callouts above them, and three-line captions below. The title sits above the timeline at top centre of the right zone, text is centre-aligned, margins are roughly 2 to 6 percent, and there is no footer, page number or logo.

**Visuals**

Photograph 1 (QPU): a dilution refrigerator, a cylindrical gold-and-copper stack of tiered plates with hanging wiring and a cold-plate base, shown on a near-black background under a white-grey mounting ring at the top. It is lit from within by warm amber light and sits in a rounded, gold-bordered frame (about 26 by 73 percent of the slide). It is not cropped, and it is a boxed image, not full-bleed. Photograph 2 (GB200 NVL72): a tall, dark server rack seen front-on, with horizontal gold-edged compute trays and dense rows of small black modules with fine gold edges, lit warmly from the sides. It is cropped at the top and bottom edges and the bottom of the rack ends on a dark plinth. Timeline: a thin gold horizontal line runs across the five icons at about y 78 percent. Five circular icons sit on it, each with a thin gold ring. Icon 1 (cuQuantum, Quantum Algorithm Development): a white-line quantum circuit diagram on black with boxed gates labelled Rx(0.107), Rz(0.699), Ry(0.8888) and X markers. Icon 2 (cuQuantum, Qubit Design EDA): a purple and blue isometric chip or qubit layout tilted on a dark ground. Icon 3 (cuQuantum, Quantum Data Generation): a small code screenshot on dark ground with green and yellow syntax text, including a line reading rng = np.random.default_rng. Icon 4 (CUDA-Q, Hybrid Applications): a dark isometric building with blue and green glowing outline paths. Icon 5 (CUDA-Q, Quantum Error Correction): a near-black disc with blue and teal waveforms and two clock-like dials. Above the icons are five values, each with a thin gold underline and a thin gold upward arrow with a small arrowhead: Speedup vs. CPU 800X (arrow from icon 1, the callout has the two-line label Speedup / vs. CPU above it), 1,200X (icon 2), 4,000X (icon 3, the tallest arrow, reaching about y 17 percent), 1,300X (icon 4), and 500X (icon 5). The arrows are about 1 to 2 percent of the slide wide, pale gold, and rise from the top of each icon ring.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (a full-slide black image layer covers a white #FFFFFF fill beneath it).

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with no title placeholder and no footer or slide number. Five stages run in a rising diagonal from bottom-left to top-right, each stage a gold heading with white caps labels beneath it, left-aligned to its icon or the arrow. The stage groups sit on a sweeping arrow that links them, and the composition leaves the upper-left two-thirds empty.

**Visuals**

Five illustrations and one connecting arrow, all hand-drawn white line sketches with a soft glow on a black field. (1) Above the AlexNet label, a small sketch of stacked server racks with cable bundles, white line on black, about 11.8% from left and 75.6% from top, about 8.3% wide by 4.9% tall. (2) Below it, a sketch of a glowing gold-tinted cylindrical or lens-like device with a central circle, rendered in dark olive and gold tones, boxed in a white rounded outline, about 11.4% from left and 82.7% from top, about 9.1% wide by 5.6% tall. (3) A single white line arrow starts as a horizontal stroke at about 22% from left and 85.5% from top, runs to about 28.5% from left, then continues as a diagonal curved stroke rising through PERCEPTION AI and GENERATIVE AI and through the halo of the AGENTIC AI figure, ending in a solid white arrowhead at about 76% from left and 36.5% from top, pointing up and right. (4) A dim grey circular halo, about 59.5% from left and 40% from top, 13.5% wide by 27% tall, behind the AGENTIC AI figure. (5) A cartoon 3D-style avatar of an older man with grey swept-back hair, dark-framed glasses, a black headset with boom microphone, white open-collar shirt, gesturing with both hands, shown from the waist up, centred, in a thin white circular ring outline, about 62.8% from left and 44.4% from top, about 7.4% wide by 16.3% tall; lighting is soft frontal, colours are white, grey and skin tones on black. (6) A robot figure in a white thin-line circle, about 77.8% from left and 8.4% from top, 11.6% wide by 21% tall: a black humanoid torso with a rounded head bearing a blue circular light, two articulated arms, a central chest panel with a small blue light, and a faint blue accent; lighting is cool rim light, dominant colours black, grey and blue. No photograph, chart, or table.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed photographic-style artwork image covering the whole slide, with a white #FFFFFF fill beneath it fully covered. Minimal dark-mode look. Titles are in a thin, light, widely spaced gold sans-serif in all caps, roughly #C8B06A; labels and body are white all-caps light sans-serif, #FFFFFF. Accent is the gold #C8B06A used for headings and the white arrow for line work. Title text is about 3.0% of slide height, label text about 1.7%, and the quoted caption about 2.5%. Line style is hand-drawn sketch strokes in white, with a soft white glow on the robot, avatar ring and arrow; no shadows, gradients or corner radii on text blocks, only the rounded outline on the AlexNet device. Typeface resembles a light geometric sans such as Josefin Sans Light or similar, weight light, all caps.

### Slide 7

**Text**

_(no text on this slide)_

**Layout**

Full-bleed single image covering the entire 16:9 slide with no title, body text, footer or slide number; the composition is a landscape image with the dragon's head in the upper-left third and a small standing figure on a rock in the lower-centre.

**Visuals**

One photographic-style CGI illustration covering the whole slide. Subject: a large golden-brown horned dragon with spiked dorsal ridges, fins and a long curled tail, its open jaw with rows of teeth facing left toward a small human figure standing on a rocky outcrop at lower centre. The figure wears a dark cap, a tan top and trousers, and raises one arm. The dragon's head is in the upper-left, its body sweeps across the upper-right and its tail curls at upper-right. Below and around are eroded sandstone mesas and pillars rising out of pale mist. Composition: wide shot, full frame, no cropping of key subjects at the edges except the dragon body running off the top edge. Lighting: soft, diffuse, hazy daylight with low contrast. Dominant colours: warm ochre and rust browns on the dragon and rocks, with a pale blue-grey haze. Two red-pink thin lines (horns or whiskers) run diagonally at top-left. The image has no overlay or tint and is not boxed.

**Design**

Background: Pale misty blue-grey sky #E6ECF0 behind a full-bleed photograph of a dragon and rock landscape. No text, so no typography. Image is full-bleed with no border, frame, rounded corners or shadow. Colours: dragon body #8A5A36 to #C9A46A, rocks #B08A4E, sky haze #E6ECF0, accent red-pink lines #B23A48.

A second placed image of 2201 by 1238 px also covers the slide at the same position and is layered as the same scene, so the visible result is one picture. Dragon's jaw and red-pink lines overlap the upper-left of the frame; figure stands on a rock at about x 44 to 49 percent, y 62 to 77 percent of the slide.)

The label is Regular weight at about 2.0% of slide height. The subtitle quote is Medium weight at the same size as the title. No accent colour is used in the text. ### Slide 9

**Text**

Grace Blackwell NVL72
“One Giant GPU”

**Layout**

Single centred composition on a black field: two-line title sits at the top centre, with a circular wafer-style image centred below it and the chip render at its centre. No footer, page number or logo; the margins are wide and symmetrical left and right.

**Visuals**

One photographic-style render of a Grace Blackwell NVL72 GPU die, placed inside a circular grey-silver wafer backdrop. The die is a large square package (about x 34.9 to 65.1 %, y 29.3 to 79.7 % of the slide) with a silver grid-textured heat-spreader strip along its top and bottom edges and a near-black border. The interior is a grid of gold/brass compute blocks, with narrow rows of small square blocks along the left and right edges and a horizontal row of gold blocks across the middle. A bundle of fine golden traces runs horizontally through the centre. The wafer circle (about x 28.9 to 71.1 %, y 16.9 to 92.2 %) shows brushed chrome swirls with bright white highlights in the upper and lower areas and dark grey shadows at the edges. Lighting is directional and studio-like, with the highlights on the upper right. The image is not cropped; the circle is a clean round mask. No overlays or tints.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a full-slide photographic image on top; the image covers the slide and is black. Black background (#000000) with white title text (#FFFFFF). Title and subtitle are set in NVIDIASans Medium, a clean geometric sans-serif, normal weight and case, about 3.7 % of slide height for both lines, centred. Circular wafer uses silver/grey tones (#8A8A8A to #F0F0F0) with black (#000000) surround. Die uses gold/brass (#B59A4E) blocks on a dark (#1A1A1A) base with silver (#BFBFBF) strips. No shapes beyond the circle mask, no shadows, no gradients other than the metallic tonal shading.

### Slide 10

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A dual-axis chart plots single-user speed against throughput per megawatt, making the trade-off across configurations readable at a glance.
- Review: Several pages cannot be read by themselves: the busy slide 10 chart with tiny multicolour labels, the small-type partner architecture diagrams on slides 38 and 42 to 48, and the dense logo walls on slides 2, 4 and 61.
- Review: Slide 10 has no takeaway title, and many slides with a title and a picture rely on the speaker to carry the meaning, so the deck is competent and usable but not self-explanatory.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or simplify the small-type architecture diagrams on slides 38 and 42 to 48, and reduce the logo walls on slides 2, 4 and 61 and the labels on slide 10 so they can be read at a glance.
- What would change the score: I would give slide 10 a stated conclusion and a clearer Hopper versus Blackwell comparison, and repeat the three UK, Germany, France and Italy stack diagrams with a larger shared template.

**Layout**

Single full-bleed chart slide with no grid or columns. The title is centred horizontally across the top at about 6.5% from the top edge. The chart occupies the middle band with the y-axis title on the left (centred at about 14% from the left edge) and the x-axis title centred below the x-axis near the bottom (about 88% down). Y-axis tick labels are right-aligned against the axis at about 19% to 23% from the left; x-axis tick labels sit under the axis at about 80% down. Series labels are left-aligned in staggered rows running diagonally down and to the right from the top-left curve. No footer, no slide number and no logo are visible.

**Visuals**

Chart: connected line chart with circular markers, drawn in gold-beige #C1B485, showing the Blackwell inference performance curve. Title: none printed inside the chart; the slide title above applies. X-axis: 'Tokens Per Second For One User', linear, ticks at 0, 100, 200, 300, 400, 500, 600, with the axis extending to about 650 at its right end (ticks are not printed beyond 600). Y-axis: 'Tokens Per Second Per MegaWatt', linear, ticks every 100,000 from 0 to 1,200,000 with comma separators. Series 1 (Blackwell curve): labelled points are EP64PP2+PP2 Batch 832 at about x 13, y 1,050,000; EP64PP2+PP2 Batch 416 at about x 20, y 1,025,000; EP64+PP2 Batch 128 at about x 28, y 995,000; EP64PP4+PP2 Batch 352 at about x 37, y 955,000; EP64+PP2 Batch 64 at about x 48, y 920,000; EP64PP2+PP2 Batch 96 at about x 70, y 895,000 (the label placement suggests these points, values not printed); EP64+EP32 Batch 32 at about x 98, y 795,000; EP64+EP32 Batch 16 at about x 165, y 720,000; EP64+EP32 Batch 8 at about x 248, y 595,000; EP64+EP32 Batch 4 at about x 345, y 450,000; EP64+EP32 Batch 2 at about x 410, y 295,000; EP64+EP32 Batch 1 at about x 452, y 170,000; TEP4+EP32 Batch 1 at about x 478, y 50,000; TEP8+EP32 Batch 1 at about x 572, y 28,000; TEP16+EP32 Batch 1 at about x 615, y 15,000. Series 2 (Hopper curve, bottom left): a cluster of small markers from about x 8, y 180,000 falling to about x 225, y 2,000, with the lowest points near 0 to 50,000. Highlights: two dashed grey-white guide lines form a crosshair, one horizontal at about y 600,000 from the y-axis to x about 248 and one vertical at x about 248 down to the x-axis; a second short dashed pair at about x 31 and y 100,000. Glowing pale-gold light streaks sit on the curve around x 195 to 290 and near x 30 to 45 on the Hopper curve. Legend: none. Leader lines: thin grey diagonal lines connect each marker to its label.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed background, behind a full-slide image layer. Black background with white and grey text. Title in NVIDIASans Medium, white #FFFFFF, about 3.7% of slide height, centred. Axis labels and tick labels in NVIDIASans Regular, white #FFFFFF, about 1.5% of slide height for ticks and 2.0% for axis titles. Series labels coloured per configuration: orange #FF9900 and #C1672A for EP64PP2+PP2 Batch 832 and 416 with brown #82451C context text; yellow #F5E467 and #DCC30E for EP64+PP2 Batch 128; lime #B8FF3C and #76B900 for EP64PP4+PP2 Batch 352; cyan #09DCFF and #006E7F for EP64+PP2 Batch 64 and EP64PP2+PP2 Batch 96; light blue #82CAFF, #3D9FE8 and #0071C5 for the EP64+EP32 rows; dark blue #003962 for MTP On; purple #8720BA and #5D1682 for TEP4+EP32; magenta #E91598 and #B01073 for TEP8+EP32; light grey #F2F2F2 and #CDCDCD for TEP16+EP32. Hopper label in Medium weight, #B5A86F. Curve and markers gold #C1B485. Leader lines thin dark grey. No rounded corners, no drop shadows, no border on the chart. The dark tinted rectangle behind the Hopper area uses #C1B485 at 10% opacity.

### Slide 11

**Text**

_(no text on this slide)_

**Layout**

A single full-bleed landscape photograph fills almost the whole slide inside a thin black surround, with no title, footer, slide number or text. The photo sits with a margin of about 2.3% on the left, about 2.3% on the right, about 4.5% at the top and about 4.5% at the bottom.

**Visuals**

One photograph, boxed inside the slide with a thin dark edge. Subject: two factory workers in white anti-static striped smocks and pale grey gloves, handling a grey 1U server chassis between them in a data-centre area. The woman on the left, with short dark hair, ear hoops, dark-rimmed glasses and a green wristband, looks down at the chassis, which she holds with her gloved right hand and her left hand under it. The man on the right, with short dark hair and glasses, leans in toward the chassis with his gloved left hand raised, palm facing the camera, and his gloved right hand supporting the other end. Behind them stands a tall black server rack holding rows of grey drive trays with lit blue-white indicators, and below the chassis is a stack of grey bins. At the far left is a wire shelving unit and a blurred orange-and-white traffic cone. Composition: eye-level, wide 16:9 framing, the two people at the left and right edges and the chassis across the centre, with the rack as the depth background. Lighting: soft, even, cool overhead fluorescent light with no strong shadows. Dominant colours: white and light grey smocks, blue-grey and black rack, warm beige-grey bins, with small orange accents. The photo is not cropped and is not full-bleed; it is boxed by the black surround. No overlay or tint.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 surround behind the photograph (a white #FFFFFF fill lies beneath the full slide area). Photographic slide with no typography. Background is solid black, with a thin dark edge around the photo. No title, body text, accents, shapes or shadows are visible. The photo is a rectangle with square corners.

Dominant colours: white, light grey, blue-grey, black, beige-grey, small orange accent.)

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent dark identity, the large hero renders on slides 13, 14 and 17, the Europe map series on slides 18 to 23 and the clean recap on slide 69.

**Text**

One Architecture – From Cloud AI, Enterprise AI, Personal AI, to Edge AI / DGX / Spark / NEW / DGX / Station / NEW / GB200 / GB300 / NVL72 / RTX PRO / Laptop / NEW / RTX PRO / Workstation / NEW / RTX PRO / Server / NEW / DGX / B200 / B300 / RTX Storage / Servers / NEW / Spectrum-X / Switch

**Layout**

Full-bleed black photographic slide with a single centred title across the top band, and a row of ten dark server, workstation and device products standing on a common baseline across the middle and lower slide, each with a centred two- or three-line caption and a short gold rule and gold NEW tag beneath it. Products increase in size from left to right (smallest DGX Spark at far left, tallest GB200 / GB300 rack in the centre-left). No footer, logo or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Ten product photographs, all rendered in dark studio style, lit from within and from the left, on a pure black background. Left to right: (1) DGX Spark, a small gold-accented desktop box about 6.5% wide, shown in closed form on the far left. (2) DGX Station, a black tower chassis with a side panel open showing a motherboard with gold heatsinks and a GPU card, about 10% wide and 19.5% tall. (3) GB200 NVL72, a tall black rack, about 15.5% wide and 69% tall, with its front door open showing horizontal gold-lit compute trays and gold cable loops at the top. (4) GB300 rack, a narrower black rack with its door open showing dense rows of black networking and compute modules with small amber indicator lights, about 10% wide. (5) RTX PRO Laptop, an open black laptop with a grey-blue keyboard, about 7.5% wide and 7% tall, sitting on the baseline. (6) RTX PRO Workstation, a black tower with side panel open showing a gold-tinted GPU and stacked gold drive bays, about 8% wide and 16.5% tall. (7) RTX PRO Server, a black rack with open door showing four stacked grey server units and a vertical strip of amber lights. (8) DGX B200 / B300, a black rack with open door showing two large gold-brown textured blocks (gold-tinted modules) stacked in the middle, about 8.5% wide and 50% tall. (9) RTX Storage Servers, a black rack with open door showing stacked grey storage drives and sleds. (10) Spectrum-X Switch, a tall black rack with its door open showing rows of dark ports and a thin vertical gold light strip, about 7.5% wide and 49.5% tall. Racks are shown in three-quarter perspective, lit by soft warm gold highlights against black, with no cropping of the products; each sits on a dark plinth. Overlay: none.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a full-slide product photograph covering it (the underlying fill is white #FFFFFF but fully covered). Pure black background with no gradient. Title is white NVIDIASans Medium sans-serif, sentence-case, about 3.7% of slide height, centred. Captions are white NVIDIASans Regular sans-serif about 1.48% of slide height, centred, with short Medium-weight NEW tags in gold #C1B485. Short thin light-grey rules (about 0.1% tall) sit beneath captions that have NEW tags. Products are photographed with gold #C1B485 accents, grey-black metal #1A1A1A and warm amber lights. No corner radii, shadows or frames on text.

### Slide 15

**Text**

NVIDIA RTX PRO Server / Enterprise AI and Industrial AI Computing Platform / Available Now / CISCO / DELL Technologies / Hewlett Packard Enterprise / Lenovo / ADVANTECH / ASRock Rack / ASUS / COMPAL / EVIDEN / FOXCONN HON HAI TECHNOLOGY GROUP / GIGABYTE / Inventec / MiTAC Computing / msi / PEGATRON / QCT / SUPERMICRO / wistron / wiwynn

**Layout**

Two-column split on a black field: a large angled server illustration fills the left half (about x 3.5 to 42.6 percent), and a centred text block with the title, subtitle, 'Available Now' heading and a 4-column by 5-row logo grid sits in the right half (about x 47 to 93 percent). The title block is centred above the logo grid, with no footer, slide number or logo bar.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed black background photograph of an NVIDIA RTX PRO Server 4U chassis, shown at an angle from the upper left, with a dark grey-blue sheet-metal chassis. Above the chassis stand eight gold-bronze RTX PRO graphics cards in a row, angled upright and fanned to the right. A black printed-circuit midplane board with gold-tinted heatsinks and eight small black cards sits in the middle. The lower front holds rows of black hot-swap drive bays with mesh grilles and latches, and fan modules sit between them. The subject is lit from above and the left, with a neutral white highlight on the chassis edges and dark shadows underneath. Framing is three-quarter view, unboxed and uncropped, sitting on the black background with no overlay. Dominant colours are black, gunmetal grey #3A4150 and gold-bronze #C1B485. Right side: 19 white partner logos in a grid, listed in the text order above, as flat monochrome white marks, except the Lenovo mark, which is a white rectangle with black lettering.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed photograph background, which sits over a white #FFFFFF fill covering the slide. Black background #000000 with no gradient or texture. Title 'NVIDIA RTX PRO Server' in gold #C1B485, NVIDIASans Medium (geometric sans-serif), about 3.7 percent of slide height, with normal capitals and no letter-spacing. Subtitle in white #FFFFFF, NVIDIASans Regular, about 3.0 percent of slide height. 'Available Now' in gold #C1B485, NVIDIASans Medium, about 3.0 percent. Logos in white #FFFFFF, with no outlines or shadows, except the Lenovo white box. Shapes are sharp-cornered with no shadows. No accent lines or rules.

### Slide 16

**Text**

A NEW INDUSTRIAL REVOLUTION
AI FACTORY
$100T
TOKENS
"UNITS OF INTELLIGENCE"
SCIENCE
ENTERPRISE
HEALTHCARE
MANUFACTURING

**Layout**

A centred title sits near the top of the slide at about 16% from the top. Two labels sit on one horizontal line at about 50% height: "AI FACTORY" at the upper left and "$100T" at the upper right. Below them a single row runs left to right across the lower half at about 60% to 80% height: a factory sketch, a token label with a squares row and arrow, then four icons, each with a label beneath. Side margins are about 1% to 4%. There is no footer and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Hand-drawn chalk-style line illustrations in white on black, with gold-yellow painted highlights. (1) A factory building drawn as a box with a pitched roof, two door or window rectangles and a gold brush-stroke glow across the upper part of the wall. (2) A row of about 14 small outlined squares above a white arrow pointing right, with the token label above them. (3) Science: a white atom icon with three elliptical orbits and small gold electron dots around a central nucleus. (4) Enterprise: a dark grey rounded robot head with a white visor face and gold glow, with a stack of gold-lit slanted document sheets extending to its left. (5) Healthcare: a white outlined twisted ribbon or helix shape with a gold glow in its centre. (6) Manufacturing: a white outlined robotic arm with a gold-lit joint, holding a small box. (7) A white outlined car, side view, with a gold glow along its body. No photographs, no logos, no charts. All illustrations are unboxed and on a black ground.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed photographic image layer covering the whole slide. Black background #000000 with white #FFFFFF line art and text, and a gold-yellow accent #D4B24C with soft glow. The lettering is hand-drawn, rounded, uppercase sans-serif, light weight, with normal letter spacing. Title cap height is about 3.7% of slide height (em about 5.3%). Labels have cap height about 3.4% to 4.9% of slide height (em about 4.5%). Token text is smaller, about 2.5% em. Line strokes are thin white and sketchy with doubled lines. No shapes have corner radii, shadows or gradient fills, apart from the soft gold glow.

### Slide 17

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent dark identity, the large hero renders on slides 13, 14 and 17, the Europe map series on slides 18 to 23 and the clean recap on slide 69.

**Text**

Stargate / 200 MW, Scaling to 1.2 GW / Abilene, Texas / Courtesy of Crusoe

**Layout**

Full-bleed aerial photograph inside a thin-bordered inset on a black surround. Title block sits top-left at about 5.7% from the left and 10.3% from the top, left-aligned in three stacked lines. The caption sits bottom-right at about 88.4% from the left and 92.1% from the top, right-aligned. There is no footer, slide number or logo.

**Visuals**

Photograph: aerial oblique view of a large data-centre construction site in Abilene, Texas. Six long rectangular flat-roofed buildings with pale tan roofs form two rows of three, with two cross-shaped (plus-shaped) connecting buildings, one between the upper row and one between the lower row. Grey rows of cooling units run along the long sides of several buildings. Left and lower-left show bare red-brown earth with foundations, cranes and construction equipment. Upper-right shows a large parking lot with dozens of parked cars and trucks. Lower-right shows white storage tanks, containers, trucks and stacked materials. Daylight, warm orange-red earth and tan roofs dominate, with a few white and grey structures. The image is a full frame inside a box with a thin rounded border and no overlay, though a slight darkening sits behind the title at top-left.

**Design**

Background: Near-black #000000 surround with the aerial photograph inset over most of the slide. Black surround (#000000) framing a photograph inset with a thin light-grey rounded border. Text is white (#FFFFFF). Typeface is NVIDIASans, a clean geometric sans-serif: title in Medium weight, subtitle and location lines in Regular weight, caption in Medium weight. Title and body text are both about 3.3% of slide height; the caption is about 1.7%. Lines are spaced about 4% of slide height apart. No shapes, gradients or shadows are visible.

**Layout**

Black full-bleed slide split into a left text column (about 0 to 29% of width) and a right map area (about 29% to 100%). The two-line title sits in the upper left, centred over its block, with a 5-column by 4-row grid of white logos below it on the left; the map fills the right side and runs off the top, right and bottom edges. No footer, slide number or source line is visible.

**Visuals**

A 3D metallic bronze-gold map of Europe, rendered as a raised relief with a bevelled edge, shown from an angled perspective with Scandinavia at top and Iberia at bottom left. It is full-height and cropped at the right and top edges, with a black-to-gold fade toward the right edge. Light falls from the upper left, giving gold highlights and dark shadows. Country borders are etched into the surface. Small silver-white map pins with gold-tinted bases mark about 17 cities, including several in the UK, France, Germany, Italy, Spain, Poland, Sweden, Finland and Greece. Below the title, 19 white wordmark logos are arranged in a grid: row 1 beyond.pl, CUDO COMPUTE, DataCrunch, OUTSCALE, Domyn; row 2 firebird, Fluidstack, GCORE, Mistral AI, NEBIUS (white wordmark in a rounded white box with black text); row 3 nebul N, net zero compute, NEXUS CORE SYSTEMS, NORTHERN DATA GROUP, NSCALE; row 4 OVHcloud, Polarise., Scaleway, SESTERCE. All logos are monochrome white on black.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-slide image, with a white #FFFFFF fill beneath it that is fully covered. Black background (#000000) throughout. Title and subtitle in a light gold #C1B485, NVIDIASans Medium sans-serif, regular weight, about 3.7% of slide height per line, centred. Logos are white #FFFFFF. Map is bronze-gold #8C7A50 to #C1B485 with dark shadows. No shapes, borders, shadows or corner radii except the rounded white box behind NEBIUS. Overall minimal, high-contrast, premium look.

**Layout**

Left-aligned column of centred text and logos on the left 47% of the slide, with a large 3D map filling the right side and bleeding off the top, right and bottom edges. Logos form four staggered rows (4, 3, 5, 4 logos) between about 36% and 71% of slide height, each row centred on the same left column. No footer, slide number or date.

**Visuals**

A three-dimensional rendered map of Europe in a brushed bronze/gold metallic finish with soft specular highlights and dark raised country borders, extending from Iceland-edge and Scandinavia at top to Iberia and Greece at bottom and cropped at the top, right and bottom edges. Scandinavia, the British Isles, France, Germany, Italy, Iberia and the Balkans are visible; borders are raised ridges. Approximately 16 map pins mark the supercomputing sites: silver/white pins at roughly Stockholm-area, Helsinki-area, Manchester-area, Paris-area, Frankfurt/Germany-area, Munich-area, Italy (Rome-area), Spain (Madrid-area) and Athens-area, and bronze/gold pins at roughly Oslo, Copenhagen-area, Edinburgh-area, Netherlands, Germany, Switzerland, Italy, Spain and Hungary-area. The map has a dark gradient fading into black at its right edge. Logos (white on black): BSC Barcelona Supercomputing Center logo with ring of arcs and small italic caption, University of Bristol crest with wordmark, CINECA wordmark, CSCS logo with diamond-cluster emblem and wordmark, GENCI wordmark with pixel-dot mark and small caption, JÜLICH logo with leaf/arc emblem and wordmark, Leibniz Supercomputing Centre logo with white 'lrz' box and text, CYFRONET oval logo, DCAI wordmark, grnet logo with star emblem, KIT Karlsruhe Institute of Technology logo, LUX PROVIDE logo with small emblem, MAX PLANCK Computing & Data Facility logo with emblem and caption, SC logo with caption, SURF logo with white rounded label, VEGA logo with stylised V and wordmark.

Background: Solid black #000000 with the 3D bronze map image covering the right side. Black background (#000000) with the map as a full-bleed photographic-style 3D render. No visible rules, shapes, shadows or corner radii; the map has soft bevelled edges and specular gloss. Lines of the title are roughly 4.0% of slide height apart.

### Slide 22

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent dark identity, the large hero renders on slides 13, 14 and 17, the Europe map series on slides 18 to 23 and the clean recap on slide 69.

**Text**

10X Growth Projection
Pan-European AI Infrastructure
3X+
2024
2025
2026
GPU Die
AI FLOPS – Ampere FP16, Ada/Hopper FP8, Blackwell FP4

**Layout**

Two-zone layout: a left column holds a centred title block and a three-bar column chart with its year labels and caption, and a right region (about 70 percent of the width, bleeding off the right and bottom edges) holds a 3D relief map of Europe. The title block is centred on about x 23 percent, sitting at y 9.5 to 17.6 percent. The chart baseline runs from x 12.1 to 33.9 percent at y 85.2 percent. The footer source line sits bottom-left at x 1.4 percent, y 97.8 percent. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Column chart, three bars, no chart title beyond the slide title. Categories on the x-axis: 2024, 2025, 2026, with the axis caption GPU Die centred below. No y-axis, no tick labels, no gridlines, no numeric values printed on bars. Bar heights are relative: 2024 bar top at about 77.7 percent of slide height, 2025 bar top at about 64.9 percent, 2026 bar top at about 41.3 percent, all on a common baseline at about 85.2 percent, so the 2025 bar is about 3 times the 2024 bar height and the 2026 bar is about 3 times the 2025 bar height, matching the printed labels 3X+ and 10X. Each bar is a vertical rectangle with a lime green vertical gradient from about #AEF53C at the top to about #5C8C00 at the bottom. Bar widths are about 3.8 percent of slide width. Growth annotations: above the 2025 bar, a thin tan horizontal tick at about y 49.6 percent spanning x 21.2 to 24.9 percent, with a thin tan vertical arrow below it pointing up toward the tick from the top of the 2025 bar (arrow from about y 58.8 to 49.6 percent), and the label 3X+ centred under the tick at about y 60 to 63 percent. Above the 2026 bar, a thin tan horizontal tick at about y 22.3 percent spanning x 28.1 to 31.8 percent, with a thin tan vertical arrow pointing up from the top of the 2026 bar (arrow from about y 40.2 to 23.7 percent) at about x 30 percent. Arrowheads are small filled triangles, tan #C1B485. The chart baseline is a thin grey #7F7F7F horizontal line. No legend. No highlighted points beyond the annotated bars. Map: a 3D bronze relief map of Europe seen at an oblique angle, showing the British Isles, Ireland, Iberia, France, Italy, Scandinavia, Germany, Poland, the Balkans and the Baltic states, with national borders as thin dark lines and raised edges catching light. The map surface is bronze-tan from about #8C7A55 in shadow to about #C8B98A in highlights, fading to black on the right edge. Gold and silver-white teardrop pins are placed on about 40 cities, with gold pins about #B09A5E and silver-white pins about #D8D8D8, each with a soft drop shadow. The map is not cropped at the top and left, and bleeds off the right and bottom edges. Lighting is a single soft light from upper left. The map is not boxed and has no overlay. Right-edge strip: a dark gradient image from black to bronze that shades the right edge of the map.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed photographic or texture image (4000 by 2250 px) beneath everything, with a white #FFFFFF fill underneath it not visible. Black background throughout. Titles in tan-gold #C1B485, set in a geometric grotesque sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans style), medium weight, with the title lines centred. Title lines are about 3.7 percent of slide height in size, so cap height about 2.6 percent. Chart labels and GPU Die caption in white #FFFFFF regular weight sans-serif at about 1.7 percent of slide height. 3X+ in tan #C1B485 medium weight at about 3.3 percent. Footer source line in grey #7F7F7F italic sans-serif at about 1.1 percent. Bars use a lime green gradient, arrows and ticks use tan #C1B485, and the axis is thin grey. Map uses bronze and gold metallic relief with no gradient in the background. No corner radii or shadows on text; pins have soft shadows.

### Slide 23

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent dark identity, the large hero renders on slides 13, 14 and 17, the Europe map series on slides 18 to 23 and the clean recap on slide 69.

**Text**

NVIDIA Establishes / European AI Technology Centers / to Grow Regional Research and / Startup Ecosystem / FINLAND / SWEDEN / UNITED / KINGDOM / GERMANY / FRANCE / ITALY / SPAIN

**Layout**

Black full-bleed slide with a centred four-line headline in the left-middle area (roughly x 7-40%, y 41-58%) and a large 3D map of Europe filling the right half and bleeding off the right and top edges. No visible title placeholder, footer, or slide number; the headline is vertically centred on the slide.

**Visuals**

A 3D bronze/gold relief map of Europe, viewed at an angle, showing Scandinavia, the British Isles, Iberia, France, Germany, Italy, the Balkans, and Eastern Europe with national borders as thin raised ridges. Six-plus silver-white map pins with labels mark Finland, Sweden, United Kingdom, Germany, France, Italy and Spain; each pin is a small glossy silver teardrop with a soft shadow beneath it. The map is lit from the upper left, with bright highlights on the bronze surface fading to black at the right and top edges. Labels are white uppercase sans-serif text placed just above each pin. No chart, table or logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000, with the bronze 3D map image of Europe placed over the right side. Pure black background (#000000) with no texture. Headline in NVIDIA Sans Medium, sans-serif, mixed case, centred, colour pale gold #C1B485, approximately 3.7% of slide height per line with generous line spacing. Map labels in NVIDIA Sans Regular, white #FFFFFF, uppercase, about 1.7% of slide height. Map colours are bronze-gold gradient from roughly #8A7650 to #C1B485 highlights, fading to black. No shapes, rules, corner radii or shadows apart from the map relief shadows and pin shadows.

### Slide 24

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The same stakeholder layout is repeated for each country, so the regional slides can be compared directly without re-reading.

**Text**

Startups / ElevenLabs / HUMANOID / LOTUS / oxa / stability.ai / synthesia / WAYVE / Upskillers / EY / Developers / NayaOne / KX / Enterprise / babcock / BAE SYSTEMS / BARCLAYS / BT Group / JLR / standard chartered / NatWest Group / Researchers / Imperial College London / University of Bristol / UCL / THE UNIVERSITY of EDINBURGH / UNIVERSITY OF OXFORD / GPU Cloud Providers / CUDO COMPUTE / Fluidstack / NEBIUS / NSCALE / NVIDIA AI Infrastructure in United Kingdom / CSP / aws / Google Cloud / Microsoft Azure / ISVs / Ansys / cadence / Red Hat / SAP / servicenow / SIEMENS / vmware / Compute, Storage, Networking / CISCO / DELL Technologies / EVIDEN / IBM / Hewlett Packard Enterprise / Hitachi Vantara / Lenovo / NetApp / NUTANIX / VAST / AIOps / CROWDSTRIKE / dataiku / dataloop / DataRobot / elastic / JFrog / Weights & Biases by CoreWeave / GSI / accenture / Capgemini / Deloitte. / EY

**Layout**

Symmetrical hub-and-spoke composition on a full-bleed black field. A Union Jack map of the United Kingdom sits centred (x 26 to 71.8, y 19.3 to 65.1) with five rounded outline boxes arranged around it: Startups top-centre-left, Upskillers top-centre-right, Developers mid-left, Enterprise mid-right, Researchers lower-left and GPU Cloud Providers lower-right, each joined to the map by a thin grey leader line. The centred title sits below the map at y 63.3 and a full-width rounded dark panel at the bottom holds five columns (CSP, ISVs, Compute Storage Networking, AIOps, GSI) separated by thin vertical rules. No visible slide number, footer or margin text.

**Visuals**

Chart/table: none. Illustration: a 3D-rendered map of Great Britain and Northern Ireland, left-to-right composition centred on the slide, filled with a Union Jack pattern in red, white and navy blue (about #C8102E red, #FFFFFF white, #012169 navy), with a thick darker edge and drop-shadow beneath, lit from above so the top faces are bright and the side bevels darker. A small floating Union Jack pin sits above the map near x 51.5 to 52.5, y 25 to 28. Six outline frames (rounded rectangles drawn as images, thin light grey stroke about #8A8A8A, 1 px) hold grouped logos. Leader lines: thin grey (#6E6E6E, about 0.1 percent of slide height) straight lines running from each frame edge to the map edge. Logos (white or light-grey monochrome wordmarks and marks on black): Startups: ElevenLabs wordmark, HUMANOID spaced-caps wordmark, LOTUS round emblem, oxa wordmark, stability.ai wordmark, synthesia wordmark with rounded mark, WAYVE wordmark with stylised swoosh mark. Upskillers: EY logo (white mark and wordmark). Developers: NayaOne mark with wordmark, KX wordmark. Enterprise: babcock wordmark, BAE SYSTEMS block wordmark, BARCLAYS eagle mark with wordmark, BT Group wordmark with underline, JLR monogram, standard chartered mark with wordmark, NatWest Group wordmark with mark. Researchers: Imperial College London two-line wordmark, University of Bristol crest with wordmark, UCL wordmark, THE UNIVERSITY of EDINBURGH crest with wordmark, UNIVERSITY OF OXFORD shield with wordmark. GPU Cloud Providers: CUDO COMPUTE ring mark with wordmark, Fluidstack stacked-layers mark with wordmark, NEBIUS white-on-black block wordmark, NSCALE mark with wordmark. Bottom panel (white or grey logos on black): CSP: aws, Google Cloud, Microsoft Azure four-square mark with wordmark. ISVs: Ansys, cadence, Red Hat, SAP, servicenow, SIEMENS, vmware. Compute, Storage, Networking: CISCO bars mark, DELL Technologies, EVIDEN, IBM, Hewlett Packard Enterprise, Hitachi Vantara, Lenovo, NetApp, NUTANIX, VAST. AIOps: CROWDSTRIKE, dataiku, dataloop, DataRobot, elastic, JFrog, Weights & Biases by CoreWeave. GSI: accenture, Capgemini, Deloitte., EY. Logos are not cropped and are all placed on black with no overlays.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed photographic-style image layer covering the whole slide (the underlying white #FFFFFF fill is fully covered). Black background with gold-beige section headings and title, white logos and grey outline frames. Title and group headings in gold-beige #C1B485 in NVIDIA Sans Medium, a clean geometric grotesque sans-serif, regular-medium weight, sentence case, no letter-spacing. Group headings are 1.73 percent of slide height; the title is 3.70 percent. Body wordmarks are white #FFFFFF. Panel for the bottom strip is a near-black rounded rectangle #0D0D0D with about 1 percent corner radius and no shadow; column rules are thin #333333 vertical lines. Outline frames are rounded rectangles about 2 percent corner radius, stroke #8A8A8A at about 0.1 percent of slide height, no fill and no shadow. Leader lines are straight #6E6E6E, about 1 px. No gradients on the background; the map carries a 3D bevel and soft drop shadow.

**Layout**

Centred composition on a black field with no title bar, page number or footer. A central map illustration sits at the top middle, the gold title 'NVIDIA AI Infrastructure in Germany' sits centred beneath it, and six outlined rounded panels surround the map (Startups top left-centre, Upskillers top right-centre, Developers mid left, Enterprise mid right, Researchers lower left, GPU Cloud Providers lower right). A full-width dark rounded band spans the bottom with five text-headed columns (CSP, ISVs, Compute Storage Networking, AIOps, GSI) separated by thin vertical rules.

**Visuals**

Central illustration: a three-dimensional, bevelled map of Germany filled with the German flag stripes (top black, middle red, bottom gold #FFCE00-like yellow), with a thin dark edge and slight shadow, sitting on black; the map is not cropped and is unboxed. Six thin grey (#8C8C8C, about 1 px) leader lines run from the map edges to each panel: Startups bottom edge to the map top-left, Upskillers bottom edge to the map top-right, Developers right edge to the map left shoulder, Enterprise left edge to the map right shoulder, Researchers right edge to the map left, GPU Cloud Providers left edge to the map lower right. Six panels are rounded-rectangle outline boxes with grey stroke and gold headings set into the top border. Logos are white or light grey wordmarks on black, listed in the Element list. Bottom band: dark charcoal rounded rectangle with thin vertical grey dividers at about x 25, 37.7, 61.2 and 74.5 percent.

**Layout**

Centred, symmetric composition on a full-bleed black field. A 3D tricolour map of Italy sits at the centre, with six outlined category boxes around it: Startups and Upskillers across the top, Developers and Researchers on the left, Enterprise and GPU Cloud Providers on the right. Each box is joined to the map by a thin grey line. The title NVIDIA AI Infrastructure in Italy is centred under the map at about 63% of slide height, and a full-width dark rounded panel across the bottom (about 73% to 92% of height) holds five logo columns separated by thin vertical rules. No footer, page number or slide number is visible; side margins are about 0.5% of width for the outer boxes.

**Visuals**

Illustration: a 3D rendered map of Italy with the mainland shown in the Italian flag tricolour (green #009246 on the left, white #F1F2F1 in the centre, red #CE2B37 on the right) and a brownish-gold bevelled edge (#6B5A34) and a soft grey-brown shadow along its lower edge. Sardinia (green) sits below the western coast and Sicily (green, same tricolour treatment only as green) lies below the toe of the peninsula, and Malta (white top, red bottom) lies south-east of Sicily. The map is lit from the upper left, with the surface slightly textured like painted stone, set on a black background. Six outlined rounded boxes, each with a category label breaking the top edge, are connected to the map by thin grey lines: Startups (white K-shaped mark with a registered symbol), Upskillers (white accenture wordmark with a chevron), Developers (white Domyn logo), Enterprise (white LEONARDO wordmark with a radiating star), Researchers (white CINECA wordmark), and GPU Cloud Providers (white FASTWEB wordmark with a swoosh). The bottom panel holds these logos. CSP column: aws, Google Cloud, Microsoft Azure. ISVs column: Ansys, cadence, Red Hat, SAP, servicenow, SIEMENS, vmware. Compute, Storage, Networking column (three rows): CISCO, DELL Technologies, EVIDEN, IBM; Hewlett Packard Enterprise, Hitachi Vantara, Lenovo; NetApp, NUTANIX, VAST. AIOps column: CROWDSTRIKE, dataiku, dataloop, DataRobot, elastic, JFrog, Weights & Biases by CoreWeave. GSI column: accenture, Capgemini, Deloitte., EY. All bottom logos are white or light grey on dark, small and not cropped. The file also contains invisible duplicate image layers at about 38-53% x and 22-47% y, with no separately visible content.

**Design**

Category labels and the title are in a muted gold #C1B485. Category box strokes are light grey #9A9A9A, about 1 px. Connector lines are grey #8A8A8A, about 1 px. **Layout**

Dark full-bleed slide with a central France map and title, surrounded by six outlined rounded panels: Startups and Upskillers across the top, Developers and Enterprise in the middle band, Researchers and GPU Cloud Providers in the lower band. A full-width rounded footer strip of five columns (CSP, ISVs, Compute, Storage, Networking, AIOps, GSI) sits along the bottom; the title sits centred beneath the map at about 63% of slide height. No visible page number, margins are roughly 0.5% at the sides.

**Visuals**

A three-dimensional France map in the tricolour (blue left, white centre, red right) with a gold-brown bevelled edge and shadow, centred at the middle top of the slide, and a separate small Corsica shape in dark red to its lower right. Six thin grey (#8C8C8C) connector lines run from the map edges to each of the six panels. Each panel is a thin-outlined rounded rectangle with a gold title on the top border and white logos inside. Startups panel: Exotec, H, a Hugging Face-style smiling face icon, kyutai, LightOn, Mistral AI, Photoroom. Upskillers: Capgemini, sopra steria. Developers: AVEVA, Schneider Electric, Ubisoft. Enterprise: BNP Paribas, EDF, L'Oréal Paris, Schneider Electric, SNCF, Stellantis, Total. Researchers: CNRS, EPITA, GENCI, Sorbonne Université, UGA. GPU Cloud Providers: orange, Mistral AI, OVHcloud, Scaleway. Footer strip logos: CSP: aws, Google Cloud, Microsoft Azure. ISVs: Ansys, cadence, Red Hat, SAP, servicenow, SIEMENS, vmware. Compute, Storage, Networking: CISCO, DELL Technologies, EVIDEN, IBM, Hewlett Packard Enterprise, Hitachi Vantara, Lenovo, NetApp, NUTANIX, VAST. AIOps: CROWDSTRIKE, dataiku, dataloop, DataRobot, elastic, JFrog, Weights & Biases. GSI: accenture, Capgemini, Deloitte., EY. All logos are white or light grey monochrome.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed. Pure black background. Titles and section labels in muted gold #C1B485 using NVIDIASans Medium, sans-serif, medium weight; panel titles about 1.7% of slide height and the main title about 3.7%, centred. Logos and wordmarks in white #FFFFFF. Panel outlines are 1px light grey #8C8C8C with corner radius about 1.5% of slide height and no fill. The footer strip is a very dark grey #0D0D0D rounded rectangle with a 0% shadow and thin vertical dividers in dark grey #2A2A2A between columns. Connector lines are 1px grey #8C8C8C.

### Slide 28

**Text**

Schneider
Electric
NVIDIA
Planning | Concept / Reference Designs
1st Fully Integrated / AI Factory
EcoDataCenter
DeepL
Electrical Digital / Twin Simulation
Next Gen / DC Architectures
EcoStruxure IT Design CFD
Build

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with a centred composition: Schneider Electric logo at upper left and NVIDIA logo at upper right, separated by a thin vertical divider at about 50% width. Five framed image tiles sit in a loose, staggered arc around the central data-centre photograph: Planning (upper left), Electrical Digital Twin (lower left), EcoStruxure IT Design CFD (bottom centre-left), Build (bottom centre-right), Next Gen DC Architectures (right, lower) and 1st Fully Integrated AI Factory (upper right). Each tile has a caption centred beneath it. No title, footer, slide number or date is visible; margins are roughly 6% left and right and 9% top.

**Visuals**

Central backdrop: a full-bleed grayscale photograph rendered as white-on-black line art of a data-centre hall, showing rows of tall server racks with cable bundles running across the top, dark cabinets in the centre and gold/tan busbar-like panels to the right. Brightness is highest in the upper middle and the image fades to black at the top and bottom edges. Tile 1 (Planning | Concept Reference Designs): a light-grey isometric 3D rendering of an electrical switchboard/distribution unit with terminal blocks and breakers on a white background. Tile 2 (Electrical Digital Twin Simulation): a black-background single-line electrical diagram with red, green and blue equipment symbols, plus a row of grey switchgear cabinets in the lower left. Tile 3 (1st Fully Integrated AI Factory): a daytime aerial photograph of a large single-storey campus of grey and white industrial buildings with a forest and hills behind, lit by low golden-orange sunlight from the left, with a warm orange tint across the sky and a brown-green foreground; overlaid with the EcoDataCenter logo at lower left and the DeepL logo at lower right. Tile 4 (EcoStruxure IT Design CFD): a light-grey transparent 3D model of a room with black server blocks, red and blue airflow streamlines and a grey floor grid, on a white field. Tile 5 (Build): a 3D rendering of a prefabricated data-centre module with a white-walled interior, dark grey cabinets and a green stripe, shown on a black background at an angle. Tile 6 (Next Gen DC Architectures): a dark grey 3D rendering of a tall server rack/cabinet in front view on a black background. Connectors: thin grey orthogonal lines with small grey dots at junctions link each tile to the central photograph.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a full-bleed grayscale data-centre line-art photograph behind the content.

There is no footer, no slide number, no logo and no margin graphic.

Scatter-and-trend-line chart with no chart title inside the plot. Y axis labelled MMLU, linear, range 60 to 100, ticks at 60, 70, 80, 90 and 100. Three straight trend lines: a blue line from about (2021.7, 70.0) to about (2025.0, 94.8); a green line from about (2021.8, 69.2) to about (2025.0, 94.0); a gold line from about (2022.6, 64.8) to about (2024.9, 88.8). ### Slide 31

**Text**

NVIDIA Nemotron Further Advances Leading Open Models
Open Frontier / Model
NEURAL / ARCHITECTURE / SEARCH
KNOWLEDGE / DISTILLATION
CONTINUOUS / PRE-TRAINING
SYNTHETIC TRAINING / DATA GENERATION
DATA CURATION
SUPERVISED / FINE-TUNING
IMAGE / UNDERSTANDING
SAFETY / IMPROVEMENT
REINFORCEMENT LEARNING
LONG CONTEXT / EXTENSION
PACKAGE AS NIM
NVIDIA Nemotron

**Layout**

A single centred headline sits near the top of the slide at about 9.5% from the top. Below it, a left-to-right flow runs across the middle band: a hexagonal icon and label at the left edge (about 8 to 12% from the left), a wavy green path with eleven stage labels in alternating positions above and below the line, and a green cube icon with label at the right edge (about 84 to 90% from the left). The composition has no footer, page number or logo beyond the two icons; margins are generous on all sides.

**Visuals**

One diagram, a process flow drawn as a single continuous smooth wave-shaped line with a sketchy, hand-drawn quality. It starts at the left as a faint dark-green lead-in from the Open Frontier Model icon, rises to a peak, drops to a trough, climbs, drops, climbs again and ends in an arrowhead pointing right into the NVIDIA Nemotron icon. Small circular dots mark eleven stages along the line in order: Neural Architecture Search (upper left peak slope), Knowledge Distillation, Continuous Pre-Training (lower slope), Synthetic Training Data Generation (lower trough left), Data Curation, Supervised Fine-Tuning (upper peak), Image Understanding, Safety Improvement (lower trough), Reinforcement Learning, Long Context Extension, Package as NIM (final upper peak). Dots and the early segments are dark olive green (about #3E5F12); the later segments from Data Curation onward and the final run to the arrow are brighter NVIDIA green (about #76B900) with a glow-free solid stroke about 0.4% of slide height wide. The arrowhead is a solid bright green triangle. Left icon: a white outlined hexagon with a small molecular/neural-network graph of nodes and connecting lines inside, a short vertical tick at its bottom vertex; it is on black, unboxed and uncropped. Right icon: a glossy green 3D cube-like badge with a dark grey network-node graph on its top face and a darker green lower base, unboxed, uncropped, lit from the top-left with highlights. No photographs, no overlays.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 background; a full-slide image covers the slide, and a #FFFFFF fill sits beneath it but is not visible.

**Layout**

Single bar-chart slide with no visible grid. The title is centred across the top at about 9.5% from the top edge. The chart occupies the centre of the slide, with four bars standing on a shared baseline at about 82% of slide height and category labels beneath it. The source caption is centred at the bottom around 88% to 93% of height. There is no footer, logo or slide number.

**Visuals**

Vertical bar chart with no axis ticks or scale. Four dark grey bars on a black background. Bars 1 to 3 are labelled Llama 8B (value 24), Llama 70B (value 35) and Llama 405B (value 40), printed inside the top of each bar. Bar 4 is labelled Next Open Model and prints no value; it is about 50 by estimate, scaled from the other bars. Each of the first three bars carries a tan upward arrow starting at the bar top and pointing to a tan horizontal rule above it, with a label above the rule: Llama Nemotron Nano 4B +21 over the 8B bar; Llama Nemotron Super 49B +16 over the 70B bar; Llama Nemotron Ultra 253B +21 over the 405B bar. The Next Open Model bar has a tan arrow to a rule labelled *Nemotron. Chart title: Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index, with the benchmark list MMLU-Pro, GPQA Diamond, Humanity's Last Exam, LiveCodeBench, SciCode, AIME, MATH-500 beneath it. No legend. No highlighted points beyond the tan arrows and rules.

**Design**

Background pure black #000000. Title white #FFFFFF in NVIDIA Sans Medium, a sans-serif, about 3.7 percent of slide height. Body and category labels white #FFFFFF in NVIDIA Sans Regular, about 1.7 to 2.0 percent of slide height. Value numbers (+21, +16, 40, 35, 24) in NVIDIA Sans Medium. Accent tan-gold #BFB08A for arrows and rules. Bars use a vertical gradient from about #444444 at the top to about #222222 at the bottom, with square corners and no shadow. Baseline is a hairline about 1 px in #3A3A3A. No corner radii or shadows.

**Text**

Perplexity Makes European Nemotron Models Available in Region / Perplexity / Create anything... / Best / Selects the best model for each query / BSC / Spanish and Catalan Nemotron / Dicta / Hebrew Nemotron / Domyn / Italian Nemotron / Fraunhofer OpenGPT-X / Pan European Nemotron / UTTER EuroLLM / Pan European Nemotron / LightOn / French Nemotron / Slovak Republic / Slovak Nemotron / SpeakLeash / Polish Nemotron / TII / Arabic Nemotron / University College London / BritLLM Nemotron / University of Ljubljana / Slovene Nemotron

**Layout**

A single centred headline sits across the top at about 9.5% from the top edge, above a left-weighted composition: the Perplexity logo lockup sits at upper left over a wide rounded prompt bar at mid-left, while a tall rounded model-selector panel fills the right third with a teal light beam linking the prompt bar to the panel. The right panel is a vertical list of model names, each with a grey descriptor line beneath, with a green NVIDIA Nemotron logo overlapping its top edge. There is no footer, no page number and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Perplexity logo: a white geometric asterisk-like mark (outlined lines forming a stylised star/folded-diamond shape) to the left of the lowercase white wordmark 'perplexity'. Prompt bar: a dark grey (#222222) rounded rectangle with a thin light grey (#777777) outline, containing the placeholder 'Create anything...' in grey at left, a row of three mode buttons at lower left (magnifier icon, a crossed-lines/asterisk icon, and a lightbulb icon in a teal-outlined tile), and at lower right a dark tile with a cyan (#1FC3DA) chip icon, then a globe icon, a paperclip icon and a microphone icon in grey, and a solid cyan (#22C0D8) rounded square containing a white waveform icon. Light beam: a teal (#1B7F8C fading to black) cone-shaped gradient beam running from the right edge of the prompt bar to the left edge of the panel, widening toward the panel. Model panel: a near-black (#1C1C1C) rounded rectangle with a thin grey (#4A4A4A) outline and about 2% corner radius, listing eleven entries. The top entry 'Best' sits in a slightly lighter (#2A2A2A) highlighted row with cyan 'Best' text. NVIDIA Nemotron logo: a green (#76B900) three-dimensional hexagonal cube with black nodes and connecting lines forming a neural network, placed at the top centre of the panel, partly overlapping its top edge; a second, smaller copy of the same logo is layered nearly on top of it. Not cropped; all elements fully inside the slide.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed background.

THINKING
I/O PROMPT
TOOL USE
COMPUTER USE
OTHER AGENTS

**Layout**

Free-form radial composition with no grid and no title. A central cartoon avatar sits in the horizontal and vertical centre of the slide, surrounded by six labelled icons placed at the top, upper left, upper right, lower left, lower right and bottom, each linked to the centre by a double-headed arrow. Labels are centred above or below their icons. No footer, no slide number and no logo are visible.

**Visuals**

Full-slide black photographic or rendered background. Central figure: a 3D-rendered cartoon avatar of an older Asian man with grey swept-back hair, thin dark-rimmed glasses, a black headset with boom microphone, a light grey short-sleeved button-up shirt, smiling and gesturing with both hands open at chest height; bust-framed, centred, lit softly from the front with a dark background behind it. A thin gold ring (about #C9A45C, roughly 1 px stroke) encircles the avatar's torso and lower face. Six white hand-drawn sketch icons with gold glowing fills, each with a white double-headed arrow (about #FFFFFF, thin line, small triangular heads) pointing toward the avatar: THINKING icon of a chess rook, two gears and a scroll; I/O PROMPT icon of a speech bubble containing a '>' prompt and a line; TOOL USE icon of a screwdriver and a wrench; COMPUTER USE icon of a monitor and keyboard; OTHER AGENTS icon of three round robot heads; MEMORY icon of a brain. The top, upper-left, upper-right, lower-left, lower-right and bottom arrows are each a separate double-headed arrow.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed photograph/image covering the slide.

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with a centered title at the top. A row of eight cartoon character portraits runs across the upper third; the four centre-right portraits feed down via elbow connectors into a central toolbox, which connects upward from a central AI-Q figure with labelled capability icons. Right-hand column at x 72.9% holds left-aligned row labels for each tier. No footer, slide number or logo is visible.

**Visuals**

Eight 3D cartoon character portraits of the same grey-haired, bespectacled man in different outfits, each inside a thin circular beige-grey outline ring: (1) artist in black beret, white shirt, holding paintbrush and paint palette; (2) in black-and-white striped top, with headset, working at a dark laptop; (3) in navy suit with a small flag pin, holding a white cup; (4) in white lab coat and blue tie, holding a red flask; (5) in red hard hat and yellow hi-vis vest; (6) in blue and yellow striped sweater with headset microphone; (7) in dark suit, red tie and small Swiss-style cross pin; (8) in blue shirt with orange-rimmed glasses, holding a green circuit board. Portraits 3 to 6 are joined by thin grey elbow arrows (about 1px, #8A8A8A) that point upward into their bases and merge into one vertical arrow pointing down to the toolbox. Central element: a small wooden open toolbox with hammer, green-handled screwdriver, red-handled screwdriver, wrench and blue-handled pliers, lit as a 3D render with warm highlights. Below it a single thin grey vertical arrow points up into the toolbox from the AI-Q figure. AI-Q figure: a small grey-haired man with headset and microphone, in white shirt, hands open, inside a thin ring; six labelled satellite icons in gold-beige monochrome with double-headed thin white arrows pointing to him: THINKING (chess-piece and gears icon, upper left), TOOL USE (wrench and screwdriver icon, upper right), I/O PROMPT (terminal-prompt speech bubble icon, middle left), MEMORY (brain icon, middle right), COMPUTER USE (monitor icon, lower left), OTHER AGENTS (three small connected character heads icon, lower right). Lighting is soft studio 3D; dominant colours are beige-gold, grey, wood brown and the blue/yellow/red of the characters. Nothing is cropped; all figures sit inside the slide with no boxes or overlays.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the full slide (an underlying white full-slide fill is covered by the black).

### Slide 38

**Review notes**

- Review: Several pages cannot be read by themselves: the busy slide 10 chart with tiny multicolour labels, the small-type partner architecture diagrams on slides 38 and 42 to 48, and the dense logo walls on slides 2, 4 and 61.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or simplify the small-type architecture diagrams on slides 38 and 42 to 48, and reduce the logo walls on slides 2, 4 and 61 and the labels on slide 10 so they can be read at a glance.

**Text**

CISCO / AI Defense Training / Eval / Dataset / Safety / Models / NVIDIA NeMo / Framework / AI Threat / Intel Data / NVIDIA DGX / AI Defense / Safety Models / Cisco Safety / NVIDIA NIM / Cisco Security / NVIDIA NIM / Cisco Privacy / NVIDIA NIM / Model Assessment / Needs / Improvement / NVIDIA NeMo Framework / Model Post-Training / Llama Nemotron Content / Safety Dataset / AI / Developer / AI Defense / Validation / Red-Teaming / Candidate / Model / Model Safety / Report / NVIDIA NeMo Framework / Model Evaluation / Open-Source / Model / Meets / Requirements / AI Application Runtime / Trusted Model / AI Defense / Visibility / NVIDIA NeMo / Retriever Reranking / Vector Database / NVIDIA cuVS / NVIDIA NeMo / Retriever Embedding / NVIDIA NeMo / Retriever Extraction / Enterprise / Data / NVIDIA NIM / AI Defense Runtime / Protection / User / AI Defense Proxy / NVIDIA NeMo / Guardrails

**Layout**

Full-slide dark architecture diagram framed by a thin gold-grey rounded outline inset about 2% from the edges, with the Cisco logo box centred at the top edge. The diagram is arranged in left-to-right flow: AI Defense Training at left, AI Defense Safety Models column in the centre-left, Model Assessment panel across the upper centre-right, AI Application Runtime panel across the lower right, and AI Defense Runtime Protection with User at lower left. Section titles are bold, centred above their panels, and there is no slide number or footer.

**Visuals**

Architecture diagram with about 40 nodes. Node types: green NVIDIA NeMo/NIM cube-network icons (NVIDIA NeMo Framework x3 in Training and Model Assessment, Cisco Safety NVIDIA NIM, Cisco Security NVIDIA NIM, Cisco Privacy NVIDIA NIM, Candidate Model, Trusted Model icon, NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Reranking, NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Embedding, NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Extraction, NVIDIA NeMo Guardrails, NVIDIA NIM); grey outlined hexagon icons with four small squares and a plus (AI Defense Validation, AI Defense Visibility, AI Defense Proxy); orange database cylinders (Llama Nemotron Content Safety Dataset, Vector Database NVIDIA cuVS); grey data-chart icon (Eval Dataset); white database cylinder (AI Threat Intel Data, Enterprise Data); white person silhouettes (AI Developer, User); white circle with black check mark (Model Safety Report); white-outlined Cisco logo in blue. Grey connector lines with arrowheads (about 1 px, #8A8A8A) link nodes: Eval Dataset and AI Threat Intel Data feed NVIDIA NeMo Framework, which sends Safety Models to the Safety Models column; Needs Improvement arrow runs from AI Defense Validation to NeMo Post-Training; Model Post-Training feeds Model Evaluation; Model Evaluation to Model Safety Report to Candidate Model to AI Defense Validation; Open-Source Model and AI Developer link downward; Meets Requirements arrow leads to Trusted Model; Trusted Model, AI Defense Visibility, and the retriever chain connect with double-headed arrows; Enterprise Data feeds the retriever chain; User is connected by double-headed arrows to AI Defense Proxy and NVIDIA NeMo Guardrails. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed background behind all elements. Black background with white and light-grey text. Panels are very dark grey #1A1A1A with no visible border except the blue cyan boxes. Blue outline boxes #00A9E0 with rounded corners of about 1% of slide width. Green-outlined NVIDIA DGX box #76B900. Titles are white bold sans-serif (Helvetica/Arial style) about 1.7% of slide height; node labels white regular sans-serif about 1.1% of slide height; small sub-labels such as Model Post-Training, Model Evaluation, Red-Teaming, Needs Improvement are about 0.9% of slide height. Accent colours: NVIDIA green #76B900, Cisco blue #049FD9, database orange #E8601C, grey connectors #8A8A8A. No shadows or gradients; the outer frame has a faint gold-grey stroke about 0.1% of slide height.

### Slide 39

**Text**

Connecting Developers to Global AI Compute / Multi-Cloud | Multi-Tenant | Scalable Training | Global Inference / Global Cloud Providers / aws / Microsoft Azure / GPU Cloud Providers / CoreWeave / Crusoe / firebird / Lambda / Mistral AI / NEBIUS / NSCALE / Scaleway / together.ai / YOTTA / GPU Marketplaces / Andromeda / Fluidstack / FOUNDRY / HYDRA / The San Francisco Compute Company / DGX Cloud Lepton / Developer Local Environment / DGX Spark / BYO Compute / AI Workstation / Dashboard labels: Explore / Endpoints / Dev Pods / Batch Jobs / Nodes / Observability / Settings / Search node groups / Request Node Group / Lambda / AWS / Developer Machines / Nscale / Yotta / Nebius / GPU / CPU / Memory / Disk

**Layout**

Centred title block across the top with the title at about 4.9% from the top and the subtitle beneath it. Three columns below: a dashboard screenshot on the left (about 2% to 39% of width), the DGX Cloud Lepton cloud icon and label in the centre, and four stacked outlined boxes on the right (about 63% to 98% of width) with their section headings centred inside. A thin bracket connector runs from the centre to each right-hand box. Black background with no visible footer, page number or slide number.

**Visuals**

Left: a full-colour screenshot of an NVIDIA DGX Cloud Lepton Nodes dashboard, shown dark with a black interface, a top navigation bar, a search field and a green 'Request Node Group' button, and six node-group cards in a 3 by 2 grid: Lambda, AWS, Developer Machines, Nscale, Yotta and Nebius. Each card shows green segmented health bars and rows labelled GPU, CPU, Memory and Disk with small values. A small rounded black badge with a white outline overlaps the bottom edge of the screenshot and shows a white icon of three people above a code symbol '</>'. Centre: a gold (#C1B485) outline cloud with a gold server-like rectangle and diagonal slashes, labelled 'DGX Cloud Lepton' in gold. Connectors: a two-headed white arrow from the screenshot to the cloud, and a white arrow from the cloud to a vertical bracket line on the right that branches into four arrows pointing right into the four boxes. Right, box 1 (Global Cloud Providers): aws wordmark with arrow and Microsoft Azure logo (four-square mark and wordmark, white). Box 2 (GPU Cloud Providers), row 1: CoreWeave logo, Crusoe logo, firebird logo, Lambda logo, Mistral AI logo; row 2: NEBIUS, NSCALE, Scaleway, together.ai, YOTTA logos, all white monochrome. Box 3 (GPU Marketplaces), row 1: Andromeda logo with wordmark, Fluidstack logo, FOUNDRY logo; row 2: HYDRA logo and a round The San Francisco Compute Company emblem with two-line wordmark. Box 4 (Developer Local Environment): white line icons of a DGX Spark box, a BYO Compute tower server and an AI Workstation monitor with tower, labelled DGX Spark, BYO Compute and AI Workstation. All logos are white on black.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-slide image behind all content. Black background (#000000) with white (#FFFFFF) title, subtitle and logo wordmarks. Section headings and the DGX Cloud Lepton label are gold #C1B485. Box outlines are thin grey (#6B6B6B) rounded rectangles with small corner radius, no fill and no shadow. Connector lines are thin light grey (#BDBDBD). Typefaces are NVIDIASans, a clean sans-serif: Medium for title and section headings, Regular for subtitle and body. Title is about 3.7% of slide height, subtitle about 2.2%, section headings about 1.7%, box labels about 1.5%, and small caption text about 1.1%.
