NVIDIA dashboard: Explore, Endpoints, Dev Pods, Batch Jobs, Nodes, Observability, Settings; Search node groups; Request Node Group; cards Lambda, AWS, Developer Machines, Nscale, Yotta, Nebius with GPU, CPU, Memory and Disk rows

**Layout**

Centred title and subtitle across the top. A dashboard screenshot sits at the left, a gold cloud icon sits at centre, and four stacked outlined boxes run down the right side, joined to the cloud by a vertical bracket line with arrowheads. Left edge margin about 2 percent, right edge margin about 2 percent, no footer, no slide number.

**Visuals**

Screenshot of the NVIDIA DGX Cloud Lepton node dashboard on black, boxed with a thin grey border, showing six node-group cards in a 3 by 2 grid: Lambda, AWS, Developer Machines (top row), Nscale, Yotta, Nebius (bottom row). Each card has a green segmented health bar, a line reading the nodes are healthy, and rows for GPU, CPU, Memory and Disk with values and small grey percentage pills; the values are too small to read reliably. A green Request Node Group button sits top right of the panel. A Hugging Face yellow smiling hugging-face emoji logo sits in a small black rounded square overlapping the bottom edge of the dashboard. Centre: a gold-outlined cloud containing a gold server block with diagonal hatch marks, labelled DGX Cloud Lepton. Connectors: a double-headed arrow from the dashboard to the cloud, a single arrow from the cloud to a vertical bracket line at about x 59.5 percent, which branches into four right-pointing arrows into the four boxes. Logos in the boxes: aws (white wordmark with smile arrow), Microsoft Azure (four-square logo with wordmark), CoreWeave, Crusoe, firebird, Lambda, Mistral AI, NEBIUS, NSCALE, Scaleway, together.ai, YOTTA, Andromeda, Fluidstack, FOUNDRY, HYDRA, The San Francisco Compute Company (icon and two-line wordmark), DGX Spark (flat rounded-rectangle device with two circles), BYO Compute (tall rack server with horizontal slots), AI Workstation (monitor and tower outline). All icons and logos are white line art except the gold cloud.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 covering the full slide. Black background (#000000) with white (#FFFFFF) title and subtitle. Section headings and the cloud label in muted gold #C1B485. Body and logo text white #FFFFFF. Typeface NVIDIA Sans, a humanist sans-serif; title Medium weight about 3.7 percent of slide height, subtitle Regular about 2.2 percent, box headings Medium about 1.7 percent, labels Regular about 1.5 percent. Boxes are black-filled rectangles with thin grey (#8A8A8A) outlines about 0.1 percent of height and small corner radius of about 0.5 percent. Connector lines are thin light grey (#BFBFBF) about 0.1 percent stroke with small open arrowheads. No shadows or gradients.

**Text**

NVIDIA

Welcome
NVIDIA DGX Cloud offers a cutting-edge, cloud-native AI platform with high-performance computing, efficient AI inference and training, and a reliable infrastructure tailored for enterprise use.
Quick Access
Dedicated Endpoints
Run models with dedicated capacity and performance guarantees.
Dev Pods

Batch Jobs
Run training, data processing, and other batch jobs.
Nodes
Manage your dedicated computation resources.
Others
Observability
Logs, health checks, and auditing.
Settings
Workspace settings, and user management

**Layout**

A single full-bleed black slide with one framed photograph filling most of the slide, inset about 2.3% from the left and right edges and 4.3% from the top and bottom. The laptop-screen dashboard sits in the centre of the photograph over the laptop display, with its own Welcome heading at the top left of the screen and card grids below; there is no slide title, footer, page number or slide number.

**Visuals**

Photograph: a warm golden-hour outdoor scene on a café table. The blurred Eiffel Tower stands left of centre against a pale blue sky with streaky white cirrus clouds, with a city skyline and rooftops on the right. In the foreground a white cup of coffee on a white saucer sits at left on a weathered wooden table, and a dark open laptop sits centre-bottom; a wrought-iron balcony railing runs along the right. Lighting is low warm sunlight from the left with long shadows; dominant colours are amber #C8893F, brown #6B4A2B, sky blue #8FB8D8 and charcoal #1E1E1E. The photograph is not cropped and is shown with a thin dark frame. Overlaid on the laptop display is a screenshot of the NVIDIA DGX Cloud dashboard on a near-black #0E0E10 background: a top navigation bar with the green NVIDIA logo and seven tabs (Explore with a green underline, Endpoints, Dev Pods, Batch Jobs, Nodes, Observability, Settings) each with a small line icon; a Welcome heading; a Quick Access row with four cards (Dedicated Endpoints, Dev Pods, Batch Jobs, Nodes) and an Others row with two cards (Observability, Settings). Each card has a thin line icon: a rocket-like arrow, a hexagon with a dot, a stacked-rows icon, a stacked-rows icon, a document with a gear, and a gear. A small white arrow mouse pointer sits to the right of the Batch Jobs card.

**Design**

Background is pure black #000000 surrounding a dark framed photograph; the screen UI is near-black #111111 with cards in #171717 and hairline borders #2A2A2A. Main title Welcome is white #FFFFFF in a geometric grotesque sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans style), semibold, about 2.6% of slide height. Body and descriptions are grey #9A9A9A in regular sans-serif about 0.9% of slide height; card titles are white #FFFFFF semibold about 1.2%. The NVIDIA logo and the Explore tab underline are accent green #76B900. Card corners are slightly rounded (about 0.5% radius) with no shadows; the photograph has small rounded corners and no shadow.

### Slide 42

**Review notes**

- Review: Several pages cannot be read by themselves: the busy slide 10 chart with tiny multicolour labels, the small-type partner architecture diagrams on slides 38 and 42 to 48, and the dense logo walls on slides 2, 4 and 61.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or simplify the small-type architecture diagrams on slides 38 and 42 to 48, and reduce the logo walls on slides 2, 4 and 61 and the labels on slide 10 so they can be read at a glance.

**Text**

SAP / SAP Applications / Joule / Cloud ERP / CRM / Spend / HXM / . . . / Customer / Partner / Apps / AI Foundation – AI Operating System / Development Tooling / Generative AI / Hub in SAP AI / Launchpad / Libraries / Generative AI Hub / Trust / Orchestration / Data Masking / Harmonized API / Prompt Registry / Grounding / NVIDIA NeMo Retriever / Content Moderation / Access / Serving/Inference Engine / NVIDIA NeMo / Retriever / NVIDIA NIM / Mistral With / NVIDIA NIM / Foundation Models Provided by Partners / Foundation Models Built by SAP / Open Source Foundation Models / SAP HANA Cloud / SAP Business Data Cloud / Cloud ERP Data / CRM Data / Spend  Data / HXM Data / . . . / Vector Engine / Knowledge Graph

**Layout**

A single centred architecture diagram on a full-bleed black slide with no title text or page number. A thin gold-grey outlined frame encloses the whole diagram; a rounded SAP logo box sits centred on the top edge of the frame. Below it, the SAP Applications panel sits top-left and a Customer/Partner Apps pair sits top-right; the large blue AI Foundation panel fills the middle and lower-left, with a green-outlined Serving/Inference Engine panel on the right and the SAP HANA data panel along the bottom. Text is centred within boxes and margins are about 2 percent on each side.

**Visuals**

Architecture diagram with dark rounded rectangles and white or blue outlines, not a chart or table. Top: SAP logo (blue parallelogram with white SAP wordmark) inside a rounded box with a light grey outline. SAP Applications panel contains a wide Joule bar above a row of four boxes (Cloud ERP, CRM, Spend, HXM) and a small ... box. Customer icon (person silhouette in a dark square) and Partner Apps icon (grid of nine white squares in a dark square) are joined by a dashed grey line; a solid grey arrow runs from Customer down into the AI Foundation panel. AI Foundation panel: Development Tooling column (Generative AI Hub in SAP AI Launchpad box, Libraries box) with a grey arrow pointing right into the Generative AI Hub panel. Generative AI Hub panel contains Trust (Data Masking and Content Moderation boxes with white outlines) and Orchestration (Harmonized API, Prompt Registry, and a Grounding box containing the NVIDIA NeMo Retriever icon); a grey arrow runs from Prompt Registry to an Access box, and another grey arrow runs from Access to the Serving/Inference Engine. Two small vertical arrows (one up, one down) connect the Orchestration area to the SAP HANA panel. Serving/Inference Engine panel has a green outline and contains three green hexagonal NVIDIA-style icons labelled NVIDIA NeMo Retriever, NVIDIA NIM and Mistral With NVIDIA NIM, plus three dark bars: Foundation Models Provided by Partners, Foundation Models Built by SAP, Open Source Foundation Models. SAP HANA panel contains four data boxes (Cloud ERP Data, CRM Data, Spend  Data, HXM Data) and a ... box above two wide boxes (Vector Engine, Knowledge Graph). Logos and icons: SAP logo; NVIDIA green hexagon icons (four in total: NeMo Retriever in Serving, NVIDIA NIM, Mistral With NVIDIA NIM, and NeMo Retriever in Grounding); person icon; grid icon. No photograph.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 covering the whole slide. Dark theme on black. Panel fills are near-black charcoal (#161616 to #1E1E1E). Blue outlines (#2F8FDB) frame the SAP Applications and AI Foundation panels. Serving panel outline and NVIDIA icons use green #76B900. Outer frame and SAP logo box use grey-gold thin line (#8C7B5A). Titles and labels are white #FFFFFF in a Helvetica-style sans-serif, regular weight, with AI Foundation – AI Operating System in bold. Title text is about 1.6 percent of slide height; box labels about 1.1 percent. Connectors are thin mid-grey (#9A9A9A) lines with small triangular arrowheads. Box corners are slightly rounded (about 0.4 percent of height). No shadows or gradients inside the diagram.

### Slide 43

**Review notes**

- Review: Several pages cannot be read by themselves: the busy slide 10 chart with tiny multicolour labels, the small-type partner architecture diagrams on slides 38 and 42 to 48, and the dense logo walls on slides 2, 4 and 61.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or simplify the small-type architecture diagrams on slides 38 and 42 to 48, and reduce the logo walls on slides 2, 4 and 61 and the labels on slide 10 so they can be read at a glance.

**Text**

Sana
User
Sana Main Agent
Llama Nemotron
Plan, Reflect, Evaluate

**Layout**

A single full-bleed black panel holds a large rounded-rectangle frame inset about 2% from each edge, with a small Sana logo box centred on its top edge. Inside the frame a left-to-right flow diagram sits in rows: a top row (User, Sana Main Agent, Llama Nemotron), a middle row (Tools) and a lower band (Documents, Meetings, Emails at left, then Indexing, the NVIDIA NeMo Retriever nodes, Internet Search, MCP, Vector Search and Third Parties). No title, footer or slide number is present.

**Visuals**

Diagram on black with white serif labels and green-outlined square icon tiles, connected by thin grey orthogonal arrows. Shapes and labels: User (person icon, white on black, green square outline) with an arrow to Sana Main Agent (person icon, green outline). A double-headed arrow runs from Sana Main Agent to Llama Nemotron (NVIDIA green cube-network icon, label 'Llama Nemotron' with subtitle 'Plan, Reflect, Evaluate'). An elbow arrow labelled 'Uses' runs from Sana Main Agent down and right to Tools (briefcase icon). From Tools, a branch line drops to three arrows: one down to Internet Search (wifi/signal icon), one to NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Reranking (green cube icon), and one down-right to MCP (paperclip icon). MCP has a downward arrow to Third Parties (people icon). Left column: Documents (document icon), Meetings (people icon) and Emails (envelope icon) each have a line that merges into a vertical bus at about x 24%, which feeds one arrow into Indexing (document icon). Indexing has an arrow to NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Extraction (green cube icon), which has an arrow to NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Reranking. A branch from Indexing drops to NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Embedding (green cube icon). Reranking has a downward arrow to Vector Search (picture icon). No photograph, chart or table.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (a full-slide black image sits over a white #FFFFFF fill).

**Layout**

A single centred composition on a black slide. The DeepL logo box sits centred at the top edge, inside a thin bronze-outlined frame that spans almost the full slide. Two large blue-outlined panels, Training on the left and Inference on the right, sit side by side in the middle band with a labelled arrow between them; the four-box row and the Inference band align to the panel interiors. There is no title placeholder, footer, slide number or source line.

**Visuals**

Diagram (flowchart) with two grouped panels and NVIDIA logo icons. Training panel: two green NVIDIA icons, NVIDIA Megatron Core (left) and NVIDIA Transformer Engine (right), each with a connector line running down to a horizontal bus that joins into a single arrow pointing down to the label Pytorch Training Code. From that label a line branches horizontally into four downward arrows to four dark rounded boxes in one row: 5D Parallelism, Distillation, FP8, RLHF. Each box has a line running down that joins a common horizontal bus, which feeds one arrow down to a small white network-graph icon labelled LLM Model. A horizontal arrow labelled FP8 Weight and Scale Transfer runs from the Training panel to the Inference panel. Inference panel: a green NVIDIA icon labelled NVIDIA TensorRT-LLM with an arrow down to a wide dark rounded band containing three items in a row: LoRA, Inflight Batching, Optimized KV Cache. An arrow runs down from the band to a small white network-graph icon labelled LLM Model, then an arrow down to a dark rounded box labelled Kubernetes. A bracket-shaped loop line runs from the left side of Kubernetes down to the left side of a dark box labelled Workflow Orchestration, and from its right side back up to the right side of Kubernetes, with arrowheads at both ends of the loop. Logos: DeepL logo (blue hexagon mark with white wordmark) in a rounded white-outlined box at the top centre; green NVIDIA hexagon icons (four in total, one each for Megatron Core, Transformer Engine, TensorRT-LLM and a repeat for LLM model icon in white). Lines are thin grey (about #8A8A8A, 1 px). No photographs.

Panel outlines are blue #1F5FA8, about 1 px, with small corner radius. Dark boxes are near-black #111111 with a faint lighter border, rounded corners about 0.5 percent of slide width. Typefaces: title and labels are a Times-style serif; the DeepL wordmark is a geometric sans-serif. Heading size about 2.0 percent of slide height; box and label text about 1.4 percent.

**Layout**

A single centred diagram on a black slide, with a Photoroom logo box centred at the top edge of a large rounded outer frame. Inside the frame, on the left a User icon and Kubernetes Cluster icon sit at mid-height, in the centre two stacked green-bordered Triton Ensemble Models boxes, and on the right a tall purple-bordered Photoroom Offline Optimization Framework column holding four pills. No slide number or footer is visible.

**Visuals**

Diagram. Shapes and connections: (1) A user icon (white person silhouette on a dark grey square) labelled User at about x 13.6 y 46.4, joined by a double-headed horizontal arrow to a blue Kubernetes wheel icon labelled Kubernetes Cluster at about x 20.5 y 46.3. (2) From the Kubernetes icon, an elbow connector with arrowhead goes up and right into the top Triton Ensemble Models box at its left edge, and a second elbow connector with arrowhead goes down and right into the bottom Triton Ensemble Models box at its left edge. (3) Each Triton Ensemble Models box contains two dark grey (#141414) inner cards. The left card is labelled Data Dependent Control Flow above a PyTorch logo and the word TorchScript; the right card is labelled Acyclic Computation above a green NVIDIA TensorRT hexagon logo and the words NVIDIA TensorRT. A short double-headed horizontal arrow links the two cards in each box. (4) To the right of each Triton box, a small dark server icon (three stacked rectangles with dots) with a thin grey arrow pointing left into the box. Top icon labelled Server Type 1 with FP8, FP4, Pruning beneath; bottom icon labelled Server Type 2 with FP8, FP4, Pruning beneath. (5) Thin grey lines run from the right side of each server label to the pills in the purple column. Top: TensorRT Model Optimizer, with a line to the server and the left-pointing arrow. Bottom: TensorRT Model Optimizer with the same connection to Server Type 2. (6) Arrows from the purple column pointing left into the Triton boxes: from Python Pipeline 1 to the right edge of the top Triton box, and from Python Pipeline 2 to the right edge of the bottom Triton box. Connector lines are mid grey (#8A8A8A), about 1 px thick, with small filled arrowheads. The Photoroom logo is a white rounded-square R icon followed by the white bold word Photoroom, inside a black rounded rectangle with a light grey (#BFBFBF) 1 px border. The outer frame is a rounded rectangle with a thin warm gold to grey gradient outline (about #C8B99A). The Photoroom Offline Optimization Framework column has a violet (#8A3FFC) 1.5 px rounded border. The Triton boxes have a lime green (#76B900) 1.5 px rounded border. PyTorch logo is red-orange (#EE4C2C) flame mark with white text. The NVIDIA hexagon is lime green (#76B900) with a white node-network graphic. The Kubernetes icon is blue (#326CE5) with a white ship wheel. Nothing is cropped, and there are no photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (a white #FFFFFF fill sits behind the full-slide image, but the visible background is black). Black background (#000000) throughout. Titles inside boxes are white (#FFFFFF) semi-bold sans-serif, approximately Calibri or Lato, about 2.0% of slide height. Inner cards and pills are very dark grey (#141414 to #1A1A1A) rounded rectangles with corner radius about 0.5% of slide width and no shadow. Connector lines are mid grey #8A8A8A, 1 px. The outer frame has a thin gold-grey gradient stroke. No shadows, no gradients in the fills.

- Suggested repair: Enlarge or simplify the small-type architecture diagrams on slides 38 and 42 to 48, and reduce the logo walls on slides 2, 4 and 61 and the labels on slide 10 so they can be read at a glance.

**Text**

qodo / Coding Agent / Llama Nemotron Reasoning / Tool Usage / NVIDIA NIM / Code Search & Retrieval Agent / Llama Nemotron Reasoning / NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Reranking / NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Embedding / Query / Similarity - 'Find  similar code to this' / Reference - 'Find code using this' / Semantic - 'Find code that does X'a / Retrieval & Generation Pipeline / Ingest Pipeline / Codebase & Software-Related Content / Enterprise Software Files / Extraction Agent / Llama Nemotron Reasoning / Tool Usage / NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Embedding / Qodo-Embed-1 / Graph Vector Database / Language-specific Analysis / Class-specific Analysis / Hierarchical Chunking

**Layout**

A single full-width architecture diagram with no grid or columns. The qodo logo sits centred at the top within a rounded box, with a large rounded outer frame spanning the slide below it. An upper band holds the retrieval and coding agents left to right, a dashed horizontal divider separates the Retrieval & Generation Pipeline above from the Ingest Pipeline below, and the lower band flows left to right; margins are about 2 percent on each side. There is no footer and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Diagram. A small blue-violet monitor icon at left connects by a double-headed arrow to a magnifier icon labelled Query, which connects by a double-headed arrow to a dark blue-outlined rounded rectangle containing two green-outlined boxes. The first box, Coding Agent, contains an NVIDIA green cube icon labelled Llama Nemotron Reasoning, a teal globe icon labelled Tool Usage, and a green cube labelled NVIDIA NIM; double-headed arrows join Reasoning to Tool Usage, Reasoning to NIM, and NIM to Tool Usage through elbow connectors. A double-headed arrow connects it to the second green-outlined box, Code Search & Retrieval Agent, which contains a green cube labelled Llama Nemotron Reasoning, a green cube labelled NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Reranking joined by a double-headed arrow, and a further double-headed arrow to a green cube labelled NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Embedding outside the green box but inside the dark blue frame. A double-headed vertical arrow runs from the blue frame down to a pink database icon labelled Graph Vector Database. Below, a three-line caption lists the query modes. In the ingest band, a pink document icon labelled Enterprise Software Files has a single arrow to a green-outlined Extraction Agent box containing a green cube labelled Llama Nemotron Reasoning joined by a double-headed arrow to a teal globe labelled Tool Usage; a single arrow leads from the box to a green cube labelled NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Embedding with sub-label Qodo-Embed-1, and an arrow from that cube runs right then up to the Graph Vector Database. Green cubes are NVIDIA NeMo/NIM hexagonal icons with bright green #76B900 faces and dark nodes. A small purple cartoon badger mascot sits at the left of the divider beside the pipeline labels. Lines are thin grey #9AA0A6 arrows, and the Extraction Agent caption is three centred lines of small text.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a full-slide image of the diagram behind everything. Black #000000 background with thin grey and gold gradient outline on the outer rounded frame. Titles and box labels are white #FFFFFF, bold sans-serif (Calibri-like), about 1.6 percent of slide height for box titles and about 1.2 percent for labels. Accents are NVIDIA green #76B900 box outlines, dark blue #2B2F7A outline on the top frame, and the qodo purple #6C5CE7 wordmark. Shapes have rounded corners (about 1 percent radius), 1.5 pt green strokes, no shadows.

**Text**

aiOla
Audio Stream
Audio Chunk
VAT
Audio Service
Triton Ensemble
Preprocess

ASR

Unstructured to Structured
Spoken Data

**Layout**

A single full-bleed black slide with a thin rounded-corner grey frame inset about 2% from the left and right edges and about 7.5% from the top and about 11% from the bottom. The aiOla logo badge is centred at the top, straddling the top edge of the frame. Below it, a horizontal left-to-right flow runs in the vertical middle band: user icon, VAT audio service, Triton Ensemble box, then the Unstructured to Structured Spoken Data box on the right. There is no slide title, footer, or slide number.

**Visuals**

Diagram (architecture flow, black background, white and green line art). Left to right: a user silhouette icon (white person glyph in a dark grey rounded square) connected by a double-headed arrow labelled 'Audio Stream' to the VAT Audio Service icon (five vertical green bars of varying height, colour about #5BAA4E), which connects by a double-headed arrow labelled 'Audio Chunk' to the Triton Ensemble box. Triton Ensemble is a teal-outlined rounded rectangle containing a title 'Triton Ensemble' and a flow: Preprocess box at top, with an elbow connector splitting to KWS (left) and ASR (right); KWS feeds ASR with a short arrow; ASR feeds down via an elbow connector to Postprocess at the bottom. A double-headed arrow labelled 'Text Chunk' runs from Triton Ensemble to the Unstructured to Structured Spoken Data box, a lime-green (#76B900) outlined rounded rectangle containing the title and these items: NVIDIA NeMo Retriever (NVIDIA cube icon, green with black node lattice) with a double-headed horizontal arrow to Context Manager (database cylinder icon in a dark square); a single arrow down from NeMo Retriever to LLM NIM (NVIDIA cube icon); a double-headed arrow from LLM NIM to Enterprise System Integration (a gear-and-nodes icon) with the caption 'CRM, Notifications, SCM'; a double-headed vertical arrow from LLM NIM down to Structured Output (JSON file icon). A long thin grey line runs from the Structured Output icon back left along the bottom to the user icon, ending in an arrowhead pointing up at the user. The aiOla logo sits in a rounded rectangle with a light grey outline at the top centre. Composition is flat and symmetric about the vertical axis of the logo, unshaded, with no photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (a full-slide black image covers the white base fill). Pure black background (#000000) with white (#FFFFFF) and light grey (#D0D0D0) text and line art. The thin frame is a faint warm grey gradient line (about #8A8A8A fading to #C9B8A0) with rounded corners of about 1% of slide width. Teal accent #14A085 outlines the Triton box; lime green accent #76B900 outlines the Unstructured box and fills the NVIDIA icons. The aiOla wordmark is teal #00A384. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif similar to Poppins, regular weight for labels and medium weight for container titles; the container titles are about 2.0% of slide height, other labels about 1.3%. Box fills are near-black #111111 with corner radius about 4px. Connector lines are mid grey #8C8C8C about 1.5px wide with small triangular arrowheads. No shadows, no gradients inside the diagram.

**Text**

IQVIA / Orchestrator Agents / Data Review Agent / Literature Review Agent / Field Companion Agent / Data Agents / Entity Agent / Test Plan Agent / Validation Agent / Rx Agent / Client Agent / Reference Agent / Data Tools / Retriever / Data Integration / RLHF / Data Sources / IQVIA / Public / Client / Clinical / Medical / Commercial / Chatbot / NeMo Guardrails / Llama Nemotron / NVIDIA NeMo Retriever / NeMo Customizer

**Layout**

A full-slide black field holds a thin inset rounded-rectangle frame (about 2% side margins, top at about 7.5%, bottom at about 88.5%). The IQVIA logo box is centred at the top of the frame, overlapping its top edge. Below it, the main content runs in one row left to right: three audience icons with connectors into Chatbot and NeMo Guardrails, then four dark column panels (Orchestrator Agents, Data Agents, Data Tools, Data Sources) whose headings sit at the same height near 22 to 24% of slide height. A green-outlined band spans under the Data Agents and Data Tools columns at about 63 to 80% height. Labels are centred under their icons. No title in the usual sense, no footer, no slide number.

**Visuals**

Diagram on a black background. Left: three square dark-grey icon tiles with light line icons, labelled Clinical (bed/patient icon), Medical (pill bottle with dollar badge), and Commercial (office buildings). Three thin grey elbow connectors merge into one horizontal arrow pointing right to a Chatbot tile (small white robot-desk line icon on dark grey square). A second grey arrow points right from Chatbot to a green NVIDIA NeMo cube icon labelled NeMo Guardrails. Column 1, Orchestrator Agents: three rounded rectangles with thin blue outlines (about #1E7FD6) and black fill, labelled Data Review Agent, Literature Review Agent, Field Companion Agent, stacked vertically. Column 2, Data Agents: six white line hexagon icons with a small plus-grid glyph, arranged in a 2 by 3 grid: Entity Agent, Test Plan Agent (top row); Validation Agent, Rx Agent (middle row); Client Agent, Reference Agent (bottom row). Column 3, Data Tools: three square dark-grey tiles with white line icons: Retriever (document with magnifier), Data Integration (window with code and gear), RLHF (gear cluster), stacked with labels under each. Column 4, Data Sources: three white line database cylinder icons on dark-grey squares labelled IQVIA, Public, Client, stacked. Bottom band: a rectangle outlined in NVIDIA green (#76B900, about 1.5 px stroke) with black fill containing three green NVIDIA cube icons with dark network-node motifs: Llama Nemotron (left), NVIDIA NeMo Retriever (centre), NeMo Customizer (right), each with a label beneath. Cubes are bright green #76B900 with darker green #4E7A00 faces. No photographs. Logos: IQVIA wordmark (white, with stacked horizontal speed lines to the left of the letters) in the top box; NVIDIA-style NeMo cube logos as listed.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (a full-slide picture covering a white #FFFFFF fill). Black background #000000 (a full-slide picture sits above a white fill, so the visible field is black). Titles and panel headings are white #FFFFFF, semibold/bold humanist sans-serif (Helvetica/Arial-like), about 1.9% of slide height. Body and tile labels are light grey #E6E6E6 regular sans-serif, about 1.5% of slide height. Accents: NVIDIA green #76B900 for the bottom band and NeMo cubes; blue #1E7FD6 outlines on the three orchestrator agent boxes. Panels are very dark grey #111111 with no visible border and square-ish corners of about 0.3% radius. Icon tiles are #1E1E1E. Connectors are thin mid grey #8A8A8A, about 1 px, with small triangular arrowheads. The IQVIA logo box has a thin warm light-grey #C8C0B0 rounded outline (about 1% corner radius) on black. The outer frame is a hairline stroke with a faint warm gradient, roughly #D8CFC0 at the top and bottom edges fading to dark grey at the sides. No shadows.

### Slide 49

**Text**

SIEMENS / NVIDIA

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark stage photograph with no title or footer. Two logos sit in a horizontal row across the top band (about 8 to 20% of slide height), separated by a thin vertical divider at the horizontal centre. The Siemens logo sits left of centre and the NVIDIA logo right of centre. A large screen occupies the right-centre area, and the two presenters stand on the stage floor in the lower half. No page number or footer is visible.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a live stage event. On the left, a man in a dark navy jacket and dark jeans with white sneakers stands and holds up a printed circuit board (a server or processor board with dark green PCB, gold connectors and black chips) with both hands, looking toward the right. Beside him is a dark grey-blue cylindrical podium with a glowing white-blue light ring near its top, set on a dark stage floor. Behind them a large rear-projection screen shows a man with grey hair, dark-rimmed glasses, a black leather jacket and black shirt, smiling, with hands in his pockets, in front of a steel-and-glass triangular-grid roof structure against a bright white sky; the screen image is slightly skewed in perspective and its right edge is cut off by the frame. The stage backdrop has repeated blue horizontal light bars and angled blue floor stripes receding into depth. Lighting is dim with blue and white accent light. Dominant colours are near-black, deep royal blue and white. The photograph is full-bleed behind everything, with logos overlaid on the top band.

**Design**

Background is near-black with blue stage lighting. Logos are the only typography: SIEMENS wordmark in teal #009999 in a heavy geometric sans-serif (bold, all caps); NVIDIA wordmark in white #FFFFFF in a bold extended sans-serif with capitals, paired with the green eye emblem #76B900. A thin vertical divider line in grey #8A8A8A, about 0.1% of slide width. No title or body text. No shapes with rounded corners, no shadows, no gradients other than the stage lighting.

### Slide 50

**Text**

SIEMENS / NVIDIA / BMW GROUP / Product Design and Engineering / Secure Edge Industrial AI / SCHAEFFLER / Operational Planning and Optimization / FOXCONN / FOXCONN TECHNOLOGY GROUP / Comprehensive Product and Factory Digital Twin / Production Optimization

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with a logo band across the top: SIEMENS on the left and NVIDIA on the right, separated by a thin vertical grey rule at about x 50%, y 8% to 20%. A central isometric factory digital twin fills the slide behind five boxed photos arranged around it: two on the upper left and right, two on the lower left and right, and one centred at the bottom. Each photo has a caption centred beneath it. There is no visible title, footer, slide number or logo at the bottom.

**Visuals**

Full-slide isometric 3D render of a factory and logistics yard in white and grey line work on black, with a lit building block at centre and a row of trucks on a dark road loop. Five rounded-corner photo tiles connected by thin grey-white lines with small dots at junctions: (1) BMW Group tile, upper left, showing a light blue-silver BMW i-series electric car in a dark test hall with yellow airflow lines and a speedometer graphic, badge reading BMW GROUP; (2) Secure Edge tile, upper right, showing NVIDIA edge AI hardware: a stacked server unit and a PCIe accelerator card on a dark background; (3) Schaeffler tile, lower left, showing a bright assembly hall with robotic arms handling dark grey and green bins, badge reading SCHAEFFLER; (4) Foxconn tile, bottom centre, showing a person in a dark coat wearing a headset beside green and grey production machines, badge reading FOXCONN with FOXCONN TECHNOLOGY GROUP beneath; (5) Production Optimization tile, lower right, showing a technician in a blue shirt at a monitor displaying code on a factory line. Logos: Siemens wordmark in teal, NVIDIA logo in green and white, BMW GROUP, SCHAEFFLER and FOXCONN badges in black boxes with white text.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a full-slide 3D factory render overlaid in grey and white line work. Black background throughout. Siemens wordmark in teal #009999 in a bold sans-serif. NVIDIA logo: green #76B900 eye mark with white #FFFFFF bold geometric sans-serif wordmark. Captions in white #FFFFFF NVIDIASans Regular, about 1.7% of slide height. Connector lines are thin grey #9A9A9A, about 0.1% stroke. Photo tiles have thin light grey #BDBDBD borders and corner radii of about 0.5% of slide width. Badges are black #000000 rectangles with thin white outlines and small rounded corners.

### Slide 51

**Text**

SIEMENS & HALSKE / BERLIN (nameplate on the machine casing, not a slide heading)

**Layout**

A single full-bleed historical photograph fills almost the entire slide inside a thin black margin, with no title, body text, footer or slide number. The photo sits in a box inset about 2.3% from the left, 4.3% from the top, spanning 95.7% by 90.7% of the slide.

**Visuals**

One black-and-white photograph of a late-19th-century electrical power hall. Subjects: two large horizontal-axis generator or turbine rotors with toothed gear rims, a tall riveted steel column in the centre-left, a bent steel exhaust pipe rising at the upper right, a ladder at the right, and a wrought-iron scrollwork safety railing across the foreground. Three men are present: a bearded man in a dark coat and hat at the left, a bearded man in a work jacket standing in front of the central rotor, and a man in a dark suit and bowler-style hat standing at the upper right beside the pipe. The central man's body is slightly blurred, suggesting motion. Composition: wide landscape frame at roughly eye level, looking across the hall with the central rotor nearest the middle. Lighting: diffuse daylight from high windows and a roof skylight, with soft highlights on the metal. Dominant colours: greyscale, with near-white walls and roof, mid-greys on the machinery and black shadows. The photograph is not cropped; it is boxed with a thin light edge inside the black surround, with no overlay or tint. A small dark nameplate reading SIEMENS & HALSKE / BERLIN is fixed to the lower casing of the central machine.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 frame around the photograph, with a white #FFFFFF fill beneath it. Black surround framing a monochrome sepia-free grey photograph. No title or text typography other than the small nameplate in the photo. The photo has a hairline light edge and no shadow, rounded corners or gradient. No accent colours beyond the greyscale image.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a car body assembly plant seen from floor level, looking down a long line: yellow overhead gantry frames and grey-white crossbeams run diagonally across the top, suspended grey car bodies and car bodies with teal and silver paint hang along the centre, two workers in navy blue overalls stand on the floor, and grey wire cages on light-blue wheeled carts sit in the foreground left. The render is full-bleed, uncropped at the edges, with diffuse even lighting and no overlay. Dominant colours are yellow, grey, silver, navy and light blue. Overlaid on the lower right is a black rounded panel (#0A0A0A) with a thin gold-tan outline, containing a diagram: in the Visualization Layer column a boxed screenshot of a 3D factory model with a green-outlined frame, below it two small green icon squares labelled Factory Explorer and Nucleus joined by a left-pointing arrow, and a green ring icon labelled NVIDIA Omniverse with a line running up from Nucleus to the right toward OpenUSD. In the Data Integration Layer column a box labelled Data Integration Layer with a left-pointing arrow to OpenUSD, and a box labelled FactoryBuilder with an upward arrow into the Data Integration Layer box. In the Source Layer column a box titled Product & Factory Planning Tools containing the BMW roundel, a mu-style logo, a Revit logo, ipolog, CATIA and NavVis logos; a box titled Simulation Tools containing an ema-style logo and a Visual Components style logo; a left-pointing arrow runs from the Source Layer boxes to Data Integration Layer, and a line from Simulation Tools runs left and up to FactoryBuilder with an arrowhead into it.

**Visuals**

Photograph of an under-construction multi-storey residential building in a city block, shot in daylight with warm low sun. Subject: a tall red lattice tower crane at centre, a grey stacked concrete block or formwork unit on a red-trimmed base in the middle foreground, two workers in orange hi-vis vests and hard hats at centre-right, a third worker in orange vest at right, scaffolding, red safety rails, glazed balcony screens and grey steel frames on the buildings left and right, and a pale hazy sky. Composition: wide, landscape, full-bleed, eye level with the building facades filling the frame. Lighting: warm directional sunlight with long soft shadows on the left facades. Dominant colours: warm beige and grey concrete, red #C8102E crane and rails, pale sky #E6E3DE, white railings. The photograph has no tint overlay. Peripherals), an upward arrow to 'Trained AI Models', and an upward arrow from 'AI Model' (Task definition and model selection). ### Slide 60

**Text**

Announcing NVIDIA Builds World’s / First Industrial AI Cloud / Siemens / Maserati, Siemens / BMW / Schaeffler / Mercedes-Benz / CAD / RTX / CAE / CUDA-X / Factory Digital Twin / Omniverse / Robotics / Isaac and Omniverse / AV / DRIVE AV and Omniverse

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with a centred two-line title near the top, a horizontal row of five equal rounded image cards with centred captions beneath them across the middle band, and a row of two server-rack groups spanning the bottom third. The title sits centred at about 9.5% from the top, the card row starts at about 45.5% and the captions end near 65%, with no visible footer, page number or margins beyond the card row's roughly 9% side insets.

**Visuals**

Five photographs in a horizontal row, each in a rounded-corner rectangular frame with a thin light-grey edge. 1) CAD: a red sports car shown in cutaway with its body panels removed, exposing the drivetrain and chassis, on a road with a sea and hillside behind; daylight, neutral grey and red palette. 2) CAE: a dark grey car in a computational fluid simulation, with swirling blue, yellow and orange particle streams flowing around the body; studio-like neutral grey background. 3) Factory Digital Twin: a wide, bright factory assembly line with yellow overhead rails, orange parts bins, red and yellow floor markings, and workers in the distance; overhead lighting, grey and yellow dominant colours, rendered as a digital twin. 4) Robotics: a warehouse interior with grey bins and shelves, a green-and-white mobile robot in the foreground and a worker in a green vest at a table; cool overhead light, grey and green palette. 5) AV: a white autonomous car on a forest road seen from behind, with green point-cloud LiDAR returns covering the trees and road edges; dark green and black palette. Each photo is cropped to the frame, with a small white brand label in its lower-right corner. Below the photos, two rows of stacked server racks in rendered perspective: the left group has two racks of black chassis with dark grey slot faces and small gold-tan accents; the right group has two racks of similar black units with gold-tan light strips. Both groups reflect faintly on a dark floor. Small brand logo images sit at the lower edges of the cards for Siemens, Maserati and Siemens, a fourth brand at the Schaeffler card, and Mercedes-Benz. Overall dominant colours are black, grey, gold-tan #C1B485 and the photo colours.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000, with a full-bleed dark background photo layer of server racks at the lower part of the slide. Pure black background with no gradient. Title and card captions in muted gold #C1B485, labels in white #FFFFFF, secondary captions in grey #8C8C8C. Typeface is NVIDIASans, a geometric grotesque sans-serif, Medium weight for the title and card headings, Regular weight for sub-captions and labels. Title cap height about 3.7% of slide height, card headings about 1.7%, sub-captions about 1.5%, white labels about 1.2%. Cards have rounded corners of small radius with a thin grey border and no visible shadow. The server rack area has a soft dark reflection beneath each rack group.

### Slide 61

**Review notes**

- Review: Several pages cannot be read by themselves: the busy slide 10 chart with tiny multicolour labels, the small-type partner architecture diagrams on slides 38 and 42 to 48, and the dense logo walls on slides 2, 4 and 61.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or simplify the small-type architecture diagrams on slides 38 and 42 to 48, and reduce the logo walls on slides 2, 4 and 61 and the labels on slide 10 so they can be read at a glance.

**Layout**

Centred single-column header over a five-column grid. The title sits in a full-width band near the top, centred, with a logo wall of five centred rows beneath it. Below the logos, five equal rounded image cards sit in a row, each with a two-line caption centred under it. Large server racks fill the bottom third, full-bleed. No slide number or footer is visible; margins are about 8 percent on the left and right.

**Visuals**

Logo wall: five rows of company logos in white or light grey monochrome on black, centred. Row 1 (12 logos): AEVA, ALTAIR, AMS OSRAM, Ansys, arbe, AUTODESK, BeyondMath, BOSCH, BMW GROUP, CARLA, cadence, CEPTON. Row 2 (13 logos): Continental, dSPACE, einride, elmos, engys, FERNRIDE, FICOSA, FLEXCOMPUTE, foretellix, FORVIA HELLA, HARMAN, HESAI, HEXAGON. Row 3 (14 logos): idealworks, Infineon, INNOVIZ TECHNOLOGIES, IVECO, JLR, LOTUS, LUMINAR, luminary, Maserati, MathWorks, Mercedes-Benz star, NAVVIS, NEURAL CONCEPT, onsemi. Row 4 (13 logos): OXA, G-Plus, Polestar, ptc, rescale, SCHAEFFLER, SIEMENS, SIMSCALE, smart eye, smartmicro, SONY, ST, STELLANTIS. Row 5 (12 logos): TORC, T Systems, tblox, Valeo, VAY, veoneer, VISUAL COMPONENTS, Volkswagen roundel, VOLVO, WAYVE, wandelbots, ZF GROUP. Five photographs in rounded rectangular cards, each about 14.8 by 16.2 percent of the slide, with a small white label in the lower-right corner: (1) CAD, labelled Siemens: a red sports car cut open to show its chassis, drivetrain and battery layout, shown in a studio grey-white setting with a coastal view at top left; (2) CAE, labelled Maserati, Siemens: a grey sports car in a simulation render with blue and orange airflow streamlines wrapping its body, against a light grey background; (3) Factory Digital Twin, labelled BMW: a bright factory assembly line with yellow overhead gantries, yellow and orange parts racks, and people on the floor, shown as a rendered digital twin; (4) Robotics, labelled Schaeffler: a warehouse with grey bins, two green-vested workers at tables and blue robotic arms; (5) AV, labelled Mercedes-Benz: a white car driving on a forest road with green point-cloud lidar overlay on the trees, dark muted tones. Each photo has a thin light grey edge and rounded corners of about 0.8 percent of slide width. Bottom band: two rows of dark server racks, with glowing amber and gold edge lights, rendered in perspective and fading into black below.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a dark rendered server-rack image across the bottom. Black background throughout. Title in NVIDIA Sans Medium, gold-beige, about 3.7 percent of slide height, sentence case, normal letter-spacing. Logo wall in white and light grey, no outlines. Card captions: headings in NVIDIA Sans Medium gold-beige #C1B485 at about 1.7 percent of slide height, sub-captions in NVIDIA Sans Regular grey #8C8C8C at about 1.5 percent. Photo labels in NVIDIA Sans Regular white at about 1.2 percent. Photo cards have rounded corners and a thin grey border with no shadow. The server racks have subtle gold and amber highlights.

NVIDIA DRIVE
Autonomous Vehicle Platform
Sensor

**Layout**

Centered symmetrical composition on a full-bleed black field. A two-line title block sits at the top centre, with the title above the subtitle and no footer or slide number. The diagram runs left to right across the middle band: inputs at left, a gold processing block at centre, output at right. The NVIDIA DRIVE label and the DRIVE AGX Thor board sit below the centre, and dark car photographs bleed off the left and right edges.

**Visuals**

Flow diagram plus one hardware photograph and two car photographs. The diagram has two input boxes labelled Sensor Data and Navigation, each feeding a vertical stack of five (Sensor Data) or four (Navigation) small gold cells through rightward arrows. The stacks converge on a tall gold rounded rectangle with no label, which emits four rightward arrows into a stack of four small gold cells. A single arrow then leads to a Motion Plan box. Below the central block is a green NVIDIA DRIVE label. Beneath that is a photograph of the DRIVE AGX Thor circuit board, shown in a three-quarter top view on black, with a gold-and-grey processor, a row of teal connectors along the bottom edge, and a caption DRIVE AGX Thor. Two dark, low-key car photographs flank the slide: at left a dark grey front view of a Mercedes-style sedan with its headlight and grille partly visible; at right a dark front three-quarter view of a Polestar-style car. Both cars are cropped by the slide edges and dimmed under a black overlay.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with dark car photographs at left and right edges. Pure black background. The title NVIDIA DRIVE is in gold #C1B485 in NVIDIA Sans Medium, all capitals, and the subtitle Autonomous Vehicle Platform is white #FFFFFF in NVIDIA Sans Regular. The title is 3.7% of slide height and the subtitle about 3.0%. Box labels and the caption are white NVIDIA Sans Regular at about 1.7% of slide height. Input and output boxes are dark charcoal #2A2A2A with slightly rounded corners of about 0.3% of slide width. Arrows are thin grey #9A9A9A lines with small open arrowheads. Small cells are muted gold #C1B485 rectangles. The central block is a gold #C1B485 rounded rectangle with a lighter edge. The NVIDIA DRIVE label is a bright green #76B900 rounded rectangle with a soft green glow and black NVIDIA Sans Medium text at about 2.2%. Car photographs are heavily darkened and nearly silhouetted. No gradients beyond the glow.

### Slide 66

**Text**

NVIDIA Isaac / Open Robotics Development Platform / Sensor Input / Prompt / Action / NVIDIA Isaac / Jetson Thor

**Layout**

Centered composition on a full-bleed black field. The title block 'NVIDIA Isaac' over 'Open Robotics Development Platform' sits centered at the top (about 9.5% to 17% of height). A horizontal left-to-right pipeline fills the centre: Sensor Input and Prompt input boxes at left, token stacks and two gold panels in the middle, and an Action box at right, with the Jetson Thor board centred below the green NVIDIA Isaac bar. Two dark robot figures bleed off the left and right edges, with no footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Diagram plus photographs. Diagram: two dark grey rounded input boxes labelled 'Sensor Input' (top-left) and 'Prompt' (below it), each with a thin grey arrow pointing right to a vertical stack of about 5 (sensor) and 4 (prompt) small tan/gold square tokens; each token stack has short grey arrows fanning right into two tall gold rounded-rectangle panels (the left and right panels with a small stack of four tan tokens between them). A short arrow from the right panel's output stack (four tan tokens with four grey arrows) leads to a dark grey rounded box labelled 'Action' at right, with a single arrow pointing into it. Below the central panels a bright green (#76B900) rounded rectangle labelled 'NVIDIA Isaac' (black text) has a soft green outer glow. Beneath it is a photograph of the NVIDIA Jetson Thor module: a black printed circuit board with a gold-rimmed large central processor package showing a green-yellow die, surrounded by heatsinks, connectors and small components, lit from above, shown full-colour in a boxed rectangle with no crop, captioned 'Jetson Thor'. Photograph at left: a dark grey humanoid robot head and torso seen from the side-back, nearly silhouetted, in low dim lighting, cropped at the left edge and running full height. Photograph at right: a dark grey and black articulated industrial robot arm with a blue-grey tinted gripper head at top, cropped at the right edge, lit from the side with cool highlights, full-height.

**Design**

Background: Solid black (#000000), with dark photographs of a robot figure at left and a robot arm at right. Solid black background (#000000) with dark photographs bleeding off both sides. Title 'NVIDIA Isaac' in a warm gold-beige (#C1B485) medium-weight NVIDIA Sans sans-serif, about 3.7% of slide height. Subtitle 'Open Robotics Development Platform' in white (#FFFFFF) regular-weight NVIDIA Sans, about 3.0% of slide height. Diagram text white (#FFFFFF), regular NVIDIA Sans about 1.7% of slide height. Input and output boxes are flat dark charcoal (#2A2A2A) with square-ish rounded corners and no outline. Arrows are thin light grey (#9A9A9A) lines with small triangular heads. Tokens are tan (#B5A272) small squares. Gold panels are gradient tan-gold (#C9B98A to #8F7F55) rounded rectangles with thin light edges. The green bar is #76B900 with a soft green glow (#76B900 at low opacity) around it.

**Layout**

A single full-bleed black field with no title, body text or footer, and no slide number. A row of 17 equal-width columns runs across the bottom fifth of the slide, each with a two-line caption above a standing robot figure; the top four-fifths is empty black.

**Visuals**

A lineup of 16 humanoid robots and one unlabelled NVIDIA column, shown as small full-length front-facing grey and black product renders standing on a black background. Left to right: XPENG Iron (white and grey humanoid with exposed joints); Apptronik Apollo (white-headed black and grey humanoid); ROBOTERA Star 1 (dark grey humanoid with a chest panel); Agibot A2 (black humanoid with a light blue chest light); Fourier GR-2 (dark grey humanoid); Boston Dynamics E-Atlas (grey humanoid seen from the front with a yellow ring on the head); Agility Robotics Digit (white-headed humanoid with a teal torso and teal legs); NEURA Robotics 4NE-1 (black and white humanoid with a teal chest light); NVIDIA Jensen Huang (no robot figure shown, caption only); Mentee MenteeBot (black and grey humanoid); Agile Robots Dual Arm (wheeled base in grey with a red stripe and two arms); Humanoid HMND 01 (white-torso humanoid on a white wheeled base); 1X NEO (beige humanoid figure, plain body); Galbot G1 (grey humanoid on a grey base); Unitree H1 (blue-grey torso humanoid); Figure Figure 02 (dark grey humanoid); UBTech Walker S1 (silver and black humanoid). Lighting is even studio light with no cast shadows. Each figure is cut out, not boxed, and bottom-aligned at the slide edge with the feet cropped by the bottom edge.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a full-slide photograph layer on top; the underlying fill is white #FFFFFF but is fully covered. Pure black background with no gradient or texture. Captions are set in a clean regular-weight humanist sans-serif. Company names are white #FFFFFF at about 1.3% of slide height, and model names are pale gold #D9C78A at about 1.1% of slide height, both centred. No accent lines, shapes, shadows or corner radii.

### Slide 68

**Text**

Disney Research / NVIDIA / Google DeepMind / Newton / MuJoCo Solver / Disney Research Solver / Custom Solver / NVIDIA Warp / MuJoCo Playground / Other Simulator / Isaac Lab

**Layout**

Three partner logos sit in a horizontal row across the top third, centred on the slide. Below them, a left-hand rounded outlined panel holds a solver flowchart, a small centre column holds the simulator icons and their labels, and a large right-hand rounded photograph fills the lower-right area, with its caption centred beneath it. No title, footer, page number or margin text appears.

**Visuals**

1) Logos in white on black: Disney Research wordmark (script 'Disney' with 'Research' in a sans serif), NVIDIA wordmark with its green-and-white eye emblem, and Google DeepMind wordmark with its spiral emblem. 2) Flowchart in a rounded rectangle outline, 1px light grey #8C8C8C, corner radius about 2% of slide width, filled black. Five gold (#F2B705) gear icons on square icons with a thin dark-gold border and a short green (#76B900) underline bar. Top node: Newton icon (cube-grid glyph) with label Newton; arrow points up from the three solvers toward it. Three solver nodes in a row: MuJoCo Solver (left), Disney Research Solver (centre), Custom Solver (right). Thin grey elbow connectors from the left and right solvers rise to a horizontal bar that joins the centre solver's vertical line, which runs up to Newton. Below the solvers, the left and right connectors descend to a horizontal bar that meets the centre solver's vertical line, which runs down with an arrowhead into the bottom node NVIDIA Warp (gear icon, label NVIDIA Warp). 3) Centre connectors: a grey elbow line from the top of the right photo edge runs left and down to a vertical line at x about 44.8%, which descends to an elbow to Other Simulator with an arrowhead pointing right at the icon; a double-headed arrow connects the left panel edge to the MuJoCo Playground thumbnail. 4) MuJoCo Playground thumbnail: a small rectangle with a dark navy grid floor (#1E2A3A) and a grey-white humanoid robot standing at centre, thin light-grey border, square corners with slight rounding. 5) Other Simulator icon: a square outline in dark green fill #1E3A0E with a green (#76B900) border and a 2x2 grid of green squares with a plus sign at bottom-right. 6) Photograph (4000x2250 source) of a boxy wheeled-legged robot with a flat grey-white rectangular head unit with three lenses, one antenna stalk on the right, a white-grey body with blue trim and a control panel on the chest, standing on four dark legs on a rocky desert floor of tan and brown pebbles, with eroded sandstone mesas in the mid-distance and a pale blue sky at top. Lighting is warm, even daylight; dominant colours are tan #B08A5A, sky blue #9CB7C4 and steel grey #7E8A92. Framing is medium-wide, robot centred right, not cropped, shown as a rounded-corner box with a thin light-grey border, about 1.5% corner radius.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 (the file's underlying white fill is covered by full-slide images). Full black background with white logos and white labels. Diagram lines are mid-grey #8C8C8C at about 1px. Accent colours: NVIDIA green #76B900 for underline bars and simulator icon border, gold #F2B705 for gear icons. Typeface for labels is a clean neo-grotesque sans serif (similar to NVIDIA Sans or Helvetica), regular weight, white #FFFFFF. Small labels are about 1.6% of slide height; logo wordmarks are about 5% to 7% of slide height. No title. Shapes use thin strokes and small corner radii; no shadows, gradients or drop shadows.

### Slide 69

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The closing summary uses short capitalised headlines to restate the talk's key claims in one slide.
- Review: The route runs from hardware (6 to 15) through European infrastructure (16 to 27), open models (30 to 36), agents and developer platforms (37 to 48), industrial digital twins (49 to 60) and vehicles and robots (61 to 68), and the closing recap on slide 69 ties it together.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent dark identity, the large hero renders on slides 13, 14 and 17, the Europe map series on slides 18 to 23 and the clean recap on slide 69.

**Text**

**Layout**

Five equal circular image medallions sit in a single horizontal row across the middle of the slide, with the title centred above them and a caption centred beneath each medallion. The title sits at roughly 14% from the top, the image row spans about 26% to 56% of the height, and the captions sit at about 60% to 65%. Nothing is placed in the bottom third, there is no footer, and there is no slide number.

**Visuals**

Five circular image medallions, each with a thin metallic gold/tan ring, arranged left to right. (1) Agents diagram: a circular diagram on black with a small cartoon-style illustration of a man in a white shirt at the centre, surrounded by six labelled icons connected by double-headed arrows to the centre: THINKING (top, with a small robot-arm icon), I/O PROMPT (left, with a monitor-like icon), TOOL USE (right, with a tool icon), COMPUTER USE (lower left, with a monitor icon), OTHER AGENTS (lower right, with three small robot icons), and MEMORY (bottom, with a brain icon). (2) Robot photograph: a cream and grey humanoid robot with a grey head unit with two round lights and a green-accented body, seen from the front three-quarter view, cropped inside the circle and lit with soft studio light against black. (3) Blackwell chip: a top-down view of a GPU die with a grid of gold-tinted square blocks inside a black frame, on a silver-grey swirl background, with a central horizontal band of black connectors. (4) AI factories illustration: a black-and-white pencil-style sketch of rectangular server racks and connecting structures in outline with gold-tinted shading, on a black background. (5) European infrastructure chart: a small bar chart with three green bars labelled 2024, 2025 and 2026 (the bars rise left to right, with the 2026 bar tallest; a thin arrow marked 3X+ points up between the 2024 and 2025 bars, and another thin arrow points up above the 2026 bar), with the caption GPU Die beneath the axis; a gold-tinted 3D map of the British Isles and part of Western Europe sits on the right of the circle. All medallions are circular crops with a gold ring; no drop shadows are visible.

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed image covering the whole slide. Black full-bleed background with white title and gold-tan captions.
